For the low-frequency branch, we design a Multi-scale Contextual Localization Module (MCLM), which synergizes multi-scale dilation with spatial attention to expand the global receptive field and precisely localize lesion regions. For the high-frequency branch, we introduce an Attention-Guided High-Resolution Network (AG-HRNet) equipped with an intelligent gating mechanism to suppress noise propagation during multi-scale interactions. Furthermore, a Frequency-Adaptive Mamba Projector (FAMP) is incorporated to capture long-range dependencies within disjoint high-frequency textural features. Extensive experiments on the OCT-C8 dataset demonstrate that our approach achieves a state-of-the-art (SOTA) classification accuracy of 98.25%, surpassing existing methods. These results highlight the efficacy of RetiWave-Mamba in robustly identifying retinal pathologies under noisy conditions, offering a promising tool for clinical diagnosis.

</details>


### [56] [MaLViL: Multi-axis Low-rank Vision-LSTM for Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17635)
*Afshin Bozorgpour,Sina Ghorbani Kolahi,Moein Heidari,Ilker Hacihaliloglu,Dorit Merhof*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MaLViL 提出一种多轴低秩 Vision-LSTM 分割网络，通过双向低秩 ViL、尺度感知扫描与跨方向融合，将 ViL 扩展至解码器多分辨率，在保持细节的同时大幅降低内存开销并取得领先的分割精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Vision-LSTM (ViL) 虽然能高效全局建模，但其计算代价随空间 token 数量增加而增长，导致分割模型只能将其限制在粗糙瓶颈处，丢失细粒度解剖细节；同时将 2D 特征栅格化为 1D 序列会破坏正交扫描轴上的邻接关系。为此需要一种既能扩展 ViL 到高分辨率、又能控制内存并保留空间邻接关系的方案。

Method: 提出 MaLViL 网络，包含四个关键模块：(1) 双向低秩 ViL (Bi-LRViL)，在紧凑的正交子空间上进行推理，并通过正交残差保留细节；(2) 尺度感知 SaLViL，在序列化前恢复跨轴邻域信息；(3) 跨方向混合器 (CDM)，融合正交的水平与垂直遍历路径；(4) 统计引导的跳跃连接调制 (SGSM)，在编码器跳跃连接中保留边界线索。

Result: 在皮肤病变、超声和多器官 CT 三个基准上，MaLViL 取得具有竞争力或最先进的分割精度；同时在精细解码器分辨率下将 ViL 算子内存最多降低 83 倍。

Conclusion: MaLViL 通过多轴低秩设计与跨方向融合，成功将 ViL 扩展到解码器多分辨率，在无需高额成本的前提下实现精细的解剖结构分割，兼顾了精度与内存效率。

Abstract: Vision-LSTM (ViL) enables efficient global modeling, but its cost still scales with the number of spatial tokens, so existing segmenters confine ViL to a coarse bottleneck and lose fine anatomical detail. Rasterizing 2D features into a 1D sequence further breaks adjacency across the orthogonal scan axis. We propose MaLViL, a Multi-axis Low-rank Vision-LSTM network that extends ViL across decoder resolutions. Bidirectional low-rank ViL (Bi-LRViL) reasons on a compact orthonormal subspace and preserves detail through an orthogonal residual; scale-aware SaLViL restores cross-axis neighbors before serialization; and a Cross-Directional Mixer (CDM) fuses orthogonal horizontal and vertical traversal paths. Statistics-Guided Skip Modulation (SGSM) further retains boundary cues in encoder skips. On skin-lesion, ultrasound, and multi-organ CT benchmarks, MaLViL achieves competitive or state-of-the-art segmentation accuracy, while reducing ViL operator memory by up to $83\times$ at fine decoder resolutions. Code is available at: https://github.com/xmindflow/malvil.

</details>


### [57] [Denoised Variance-Based Pruning with Optimal Brain Bias Compensation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17657)
*Geon Tack Lee,Jaegul Choo,Kang Eun Jeon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 DVBP + OB²C 方法，通过随机矩阵理论去噪激活协方差并引入均值偏移补偿，实现无需重训练的 ViT 结构剪枝，在多个模型上以 50% MLP 剪枝率保留超 90% Top-1 精度，显著优于 VBP。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ViT 计算开销大，难以边缘部署；现有结构剪枝方法存在精度严重下降或需昂贵重训练的问题。VBP 虽以激活方差选神经元开创了新范式，但受限于有限样本激活协方差的统计噪声，且仅做偏置更新无法完全补偿结构重构误差。

Method: 提出 DVBP + OB²C：1) 利用随机矩阵理论（RMT）过滤激活协方差谱中的噪声，实现鲁棒的神经元选择；2) 数学证明将均值偏移补偿融入 Optimal Brain Compression 目标后，逐层 Hessian 精确退化为激活协方差矩阵；3) 基于此导出对剩余权重的封闭形式最优更新，且复用选择阶段采集的统计量。

Result: 在 DeiT、Swin、ConvNeXt 架构上验证，DVBP + OB²C 取得无需训练的最先进性能；50% MLP 剪枝下，Small 与 Base 变体保留超 90% 原始 Top-1 精度，相较 VBP 最高提升 29.46%（ConvNeXt-T）与 7.33%（Swin-S）。

Conclusion: 通过去噪协方差谱与带均值偏移的 Optimal Brain 补偿，DVBP + OB²C 消除了 VBP 的两大局限，在无重训练条件下实现高保真的 ViT 结构剪枝，兼具精度与效率，代码已开源。

Abstract: Vision Transformers (ViTs) achieve state-of-the-art performance but carry massive computational overhead that restricts edge deployment. Although structural pruning has emerged as a key strategy to reduce these costs, existing methods often suffer from severe accuracy degradation or require expensive retraining. Recently, Variance-Based Pruning (VBP) introduced a promising paradigm by selecting neurons based on activation variance; however, it remains limited by statistical noise in finite-sample activation covariance and reliance on bias-only updates that cannot fully account for structural reconstruction error. To address these limitations, we introduce Denoised Variance-Based Pruning with Optimal Brain Bias Compensation (DVBP + OB$^2$C). We leverage random matrix theory to filter noise from the activation covariance spectrum for robust neuron selection and mathematically prove that integrating mean-shift compensation into the Optimal Brain Compression objective reduces the layer-wise Hessian exactly to the activation covariance matrix. This enables an optimal, closed-form update of the remaining weights using the same statistics gathered for selection. Extensive experiments on DeiT, Swin, and ConvNeXt architectures demonstrate that DVBP + OB$^2$C achieves state-of-the-art training-free performance; at 50% MLP pruning, it retains over 90% of the original Top-1 accuracy on Small and Base variants, outperforming VBP by up to 29.46% (ConvNeXt-T) and 7.33% (Swin-S). The code is available at: https://github.com/geontackee/DVBP_OB2C.

</details>


### [58] [Is Haar Enough? Exploring Symlets and Coiflets for Wavelet Convolution Layers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17662)
*Md Rifat Ur Rahman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了小波卷积层中滤波器长度（F）与分解层数（L）之间的权衡，发现采用近似能力更强（滤波器更长）的小波基可在较低分解深度下达到同等精度，从而在 Coiflet 基底下以约少 32% 的额外参数量和 33% 的额外 FLOPs 匹配 Haar 深层的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有小波卷积工作将小波基固定为 Haar 或 Daubechies 并选定分解深度，未探究更换小波基能否移动效率前沿。作者旨在揭示并刻画一个此前未被探索的权衡：更强近似能力（更长滤波器）的小波基能否降低所需分解深度，从而在更高单层变换代价下实现参数量与计算量的净减少。

Method: 作者将此问题形式化为 F-vs.-L 权衡（滤波器长度 vs. 分解层数），在受控网络架构与预算下，系统比较 Haar、Daubechies、Symlets 与 Coiflets 四种小波基，并在图像分类（CIFAR-10、ImageNet-1K）与语义分割（Cityscapes）任务上进行评估。

Result: 实验表明，基于 Coiflet 的小波卷积在较浅分解深度下即可达到 Haar 深层分解的精度，同时额外参数量减少约 32%、额外 FLOPs 减少约 33%，验证了更长滤波器可降低所需分解深度并带来净效率收益。

Conclusion: 存在一个清晰的 F-vs.-L 效率前沿：用更长滤波器的小波基换取更少的分解层数，可在不损失精度的情况下显著节省参数与计算开销。Coiflet 是构建高效小波卷积架构的一个具体且可操作的实用选择。

Abstract: Wavelet convolution layers have recently emerged as an efficient mechanism for enlarging receptive fields through multiresolution analysis, but prior work has fixed the wavelet basis to Haar or Daubechies at a chosen decomposition depth, leaving open whether a different basis can shift the underlying efficiency frontier. We identify and characterize a previously unexplored trade-off in this setting: bases with stronger approximation properties (longer filters) can reduce the decomposition depth required for competitive accuracy, yielding a net reduction in parameters and FLOPs despite higher perlevel transform cost. We formalize this as an F-vs.-L tradeoff (filter length vs. decomposition levels) and study it systematically across Haar, Daubechies, Symlets, and Coiflets under controlled architectures and budgets. On image classification (CIFAR-10, ImageNet-1K) and semantic segmentation (Cityscapes), Coiflet-based wavelet convolutions match Haar at deeper levels with approximately 32% fewer additional parameters and 33% fewer additional FLOPs, providing a concrete and actionable design choice for practitioners building wavelet-based architectures.

</details>


### [59] [Differentiable Voronoi Ray Tracing Beyond Rasterization Speeds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17682)
*Bernardo Taveira,Carl Lindström,Joakim Johnander,Fredrik Kahl*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VoroTracing 提出一种基于可微 Voronoi 光线追踪的实时新视角合成方法，通过共同设计场景表示、优化与 GPU 执行，在 RTX 5090 上达到 623 FPS，吞吐量是最快先前光线方法的 3.2 倍、3D Gaussian Splatting 的 2.8 倍，同时保持竞争力重建质量，并天然支持鱼眼、卷帘快门、运动模糊和景深等效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时新视角合成由光栅化的显式基元主导，这类投影管线吞吐量高，但处理畸变、卷帘快门、景深等非针孔效应需要专门的扩展。基于光线的渲染能自然表达这些效果，但通常被认为太慢，难以用于有竞争力的实时渲染。作者希望从根本上提升光线渲染的吞吐量，使其兼具光栅化的速度与光线追踪的灵活性。

Method: 作者分析了可微 Voronoi 光线追踪中决定吞吐量的因素，确定遍历长度、每单元工作量与内存局部性为主要瓶颈，并据此提出 VoroTracing，联合设计场景表示、优化与 GPU 执行：采用紧凑的八面体外观纹理减少内存流量；通过表面集中的不透明度促进提前终止；固定预算的表示在无剪枝和致密化的条件下优化；GPU 实现针对相干遍历设计。渲染器通过光线生成与采样支持鱼眼、卷帘快门、运动模糊和景深，无需专门的光栅化扩展。

Result: 在 Mip-NeRF 360 数据集上，VoroTracing 在 RTX 5090 上以 623 FPS 渲染，吞吐量达到最快先前光线方法的 3.2 倍、3D Gaussian Splatting 的 2.8 倍，同时保持有竞争力的重建质量。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，借助光线渲染的灵活性完全可以实现实时吞吐量，打破了光线渲染难以达到有竞争力实时性能的假设。作者已开源代码。

Abstract: Real-time novel view synthesis is dominated by rasterized explicit primitives. These projection-based pipelines provide high throughput but require specialized extensions for non-pinhole effects such as distortion, rolling shutter, and depth of field. Ray-based rendering expresses these effects naturally but is generally assumed too slow for competitive real-time rendering. We analyze the factors governing throughput in differentiable Voronoi ray tracing and identify traversal length, per-cell work, and memory locality as principal determinants. Guided by this, we introduce VoroTracing, which co-designs the scene representation, optimization, and GPU execution to reduce these costs. Compact octahedral appearance textures reduce memory traffic, while surface-concentrated opacity promotes early termination. The fixed-budget representation is optimized without pruning or densification and rendered with a GPU implementation designed for coherent traversal. On Mip-NeRF 360, VoroTracing renders at 623 FPS on an RTX 5090, providing $3.2\times$ the throughput of the fastest prior ray-based method and $2.8\times$ that of 3D Gaussian Splatting, while maintaining competitive reconstruction quality. Our renderer supports fisheye, rolling-shutter, motion-blur, and depth-of-field effects through ray generation and sampling, requiring no specialized rasterization. These results show that real-time throughput can be achieved with the flexibility of ray-based rendering. We release our source code, see https://research.zenseact.com/publications/vorotracing

</details>


### [60] [Magnitude-Direction Decoupling for Fast Video Generation with Flow Matching Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17695)
*Haonan Xu,Feiyang Chen,Songkui Chen,Hongpeng Pan,Zhefeng Wang,Xinyu Duan,Baoxing Huai,Yang Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Magnitude-Direction Decoupling (MDD) 方法，通过解耦去噪输出的幅度与方向，用方向校准的轻量模型替代原模型加速视频生成采样，并在 CFG 下复用幅度信息，在 Wan2.1 上实现最高 2.95 倍加速且保持视觉质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流匹配视频生成模型因迭代去噪计算开销巨大。并非所有去噪步骤都需要原始模型，部分步骤可用轻量替代方案加速；但直接使用缓存或轻量模型会偏离原始去噪轨迹，导致性能下降。作者旨在实现更可靠、更轻量的采样加速。

Method: 通过经验分析发现：轻量模型能稳健捕捉原模型输出的幅度分量，而缓存能提供可靠的方向引导。据此提出 MDD 方法，自适应地使用"方向校准"的轻量模型替代原模型以加速推理并修正去噪轨迹偏差；同时在无分类器引导（CFG）下复用幅度信息进一步降低推理成本。

Result: 实验表明 MDD 优于现有加速方法，在 Wan2.1 上实现最高 2.95 倍加速，同时保持高视觉保真度和内容丰富度。

Conclusion: MDD 通过幅度-方向解耦提供了一种更可靠、更轻量的采样加速方案，在显著降低计算开销的同时维持了视频生成质量。

Abstract: Flow matching models for video generation achieve impressive performance but suffer from high computational overhead due to iterative denoising. In fact, the original model is not necessary for all denoising steps, allowing some steps to use lightweight alternatives for faster sampling. However, directly using caching or lightweight models can deviate from the original denoising trajectory, resulting in suboptimal performance. Through empirical analysis, we find that lightweight models can robustly capture the magnitude components of the original model's output, while caching provides reliable directional guidance. Building on this insight, we propose the Magnitude-Direction Decoupling (MDD) method, which adaptively employs a direction-calibrated lightweight model as a substitute for the original model to accelerate inference and effectively correct deviations in the denoising trajectory. Moreover, MDD further reduces inference costs by reusing magnitude information under classifier-free guidance (CFG). As a result, MDD offers a more reliable and lightweight solution to accelerate sampling. Experiments show that MDD outperforms existing acceleration methods, delivering promising speedups (e.g., up to 2.95x on Wan2.1) while preserving high visual fidelity and content richness.

</details>


### [61] [Environment-Invariant Subspace Learning for Generalizable Deepfake Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17700)
*Shenghao Chen,Hao Jia,Chen Li,Chunjie Ma,Zan Gao,Shengyong Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对深度伪造检测中跨分布泛化的瓶颈，本文提出环境不变子空间学习（EISL）框架，通过可学习的低秩投影将特征解耦为正交的伪造相关不变因子与环境相关残差因子，并设计环境干预模块生成多样化的分布外环境偏移干预对，引导模型学习真正不变的伪造表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法利用大规模视觉基础模型（VFMs）的语义先验提升深度伪造检测泛化能力，但这些语义先验易受光照、风格等环境因素干扰，进而使伪造线索与环境模式之间建立虚假相关（spurious correlations），严重限制跨分布泛化性能。

Method: 提出环境不变子空间学习（EISL）框架：①核心在于利用可学习的低秩投影，将特征解耦为正交的伪造相关不变因子与环境相关残差因子；②设计环境干预（Environmental Intervention）模块，生成多样且具有挑战性的干预对，模拟分布外环境偏移，引导模型发现真正不变的伪造表征；③在跨数据集、跨生成器、整脸合成及腐蚀等多种设置下评估。

Result: 实验表明，EISL 在跨数据集、跨生成器、整脸合成和腐蚀等设置下均取得一致的性能提升，并对强劲的基线检测器取得具有竞争力或领先的表现，展现出对未知伪造类型与环境变化更强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 本文为理解和突破 VFMs 在深度伪造检测中的泛化障碍提供了新视角和有价值的探索，证明通过解耦环境与伪造因子可有效缓解虚假相关并提升跨分布泛化性能。

Abstract: Cross-distribution generalization remains a critical bottleneck in deepfake detection. While recent efforts leverage the semantic priors of large-scale visual foundation models (VFMs), a noteworthy yet underexplored challenge remains: the susceptibility of these semantic priors to environmental interference from factors such as lighting and style. Crucially, this interference establishes spurious correlations between forgery cues and environmental patterns that severely limit generalization. To address this fundamental challenge, we propose an innovative Environment-Invariant Subspace Learning (EISL) framework. The core contribution of EISL is that it aims to disentangle features into orthogonal forgery-relevant invariant factors and environment-related residual factors via a learnable low-rank projection. To facilitate robust feature disentanglement, we also design an Environmental Intervention module that generates diverse and challenging intervention pairs, simulating out-of-distribution environmental shifts to guide the model toward discovering truly invariant forgery representations. Experiments across cross-dataset, cross-generator, whole-face synthesis, and corruption settings show consistent gains and competitive or leading performance against strong detectors, demonstrating improved robustness to unseen forgery types and environmental variations. This work provides a new perspective and a valuable exploration for understanding and tackling the generalization barriers of VFMs in deepfake detection.

</details>


### [62] [Monitoring Pasture Restoration from Satellite Image Time Series: Caveats and Opportunities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17704)
*Linnea Sartorius,Isak Randahl,Delia Fano Yela,Georg Andersson,Sadegh Jamali,Aleksis Pirinen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次将牧场恢复状态监测建模为基于卫星图像时间序列(SITS)的二分类深度学习问题,在1,397个瑞典恢复牧场数据上评估两种SITS架构,发现显式建模年内变异与逐牧场归一化可提升可分性,最优模型精度达0.88;同时揭示时间相关偏差,强调需时间平衡标签与专门评估协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模自然恢复监测是重要而困难的生态问题。半自然草地恢复结果发展缓慢,而卫星观测受天气、获取条件和处理伪影影响,难以区分真实恢复信号与无关的时间变异。作者想探究能否直接从卫星图像时间序列中检测恢复状态,而非依赖传统实地调查。

Method: 将牧场恢复表述为二元深度学习分类问题,在1,397个瑞典恢复牧场数据上评估两种常见SITS深度学习架构,并使用不同的Sentinel-2图像(波段/指数)组合。关键设计包括显式建模年内(季节)变异,以及逐牧场归一化。随后进行针对性偏差分析,检验年份相关的混杂因素。

Result: 显式建模年内变异和逐牧场归一化提升了类别可分性,最优模型精度达到0.88。偏差分析发现,可靠部署需要时间上平衡的标签,以及能够显式检验年份相关混杂的评估协议。

Conclusion: 本文并非提供一个已解决的恢复监测系统,而是一个现实案例研究,指出哪些方法有效、哪些失败,以及未来研究应控制哪些因素(如时间平衡标签、年份混杂评估)。代码与模型已开源。

Abstract: Monitoring nature restoration at scale is an important but difficult ecological problem. Deep learning methods to analyze satellite image time series (SITS) have been widely used for land surface monitoring. In semi-natural grasslands - the habitat type in focus in this work - restoration outcomes develop gradually, yet satellite observations are influenced by weather, acquisition conditions, and processing artefacts, making it difficult to distinguish genuine restoration signals from unrelated temporal variation. In this work, we examine - to the best of our knowledge, for the first time - whether restoration status can be detected directly from satellite image time series by formulating pasture restoration as a binary deep learning classification problem. We evaluate two common SITS deep learning architectures on different Sentinel-2 image combinations, across 1,397 restored Swedish pastures and find that explicitly modeling intra-year variability and per-pasture normalization increases separability, reaching 0.88 accuracy for the best model. We further investigate our results and perform a targeted bias analysis finding that reliable deployment requires temporally balanced labels and evaluation protocols that explicitly test for year-related confounding. We therefore frame our contribution not as a solved restoration-monitoring system, but as a realistic case study of what works, what fails, and what future studies should control for. Code and models are available at https://github.com/aleksispi/ml-nature-resto.

</details>


### [63] [DynaForcing: Overcoming Dynamic Collapse in Self-Forcing Distillation for Streaming Avatar Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17707)
*Yubo Huang,Sirui Zhao,Xinchen Yao,Zhengye Zhang,Jinyang Huang,Fengqi Cui,Shiwei Wu,Enhong Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DynaForcing 针对基于 DMD 自蒸馏（self-forcing）的音频驱动虚拟人训练中的"动态崩溃"问题，提出三个层面的互补策略（Hybrid Forcing、Dynamics-Aware Reward Regularization、Reference Perturbation），并配合计算图剪枝与梯度回放大幅降低显存开销，在恢复动态与同步质量的同时保持视觉质量，无需早停。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音频驱动虚拟人生成需要真实唇同步、丰富表情与实时流式推理。现有 DMD 自蒸馏范式虽能实现实时，但存在被忽视的"动态崩溃"：学生模型收敛到高感知质量但动态严重受限的近静态解。作者将其归因于两点——DMD 的反向 KL 目标偏向低运动模态，以及缺乏锚定的自条件机制形成放大崩溃的反馈回路。对虚拟人而言，即使微小的运动损失也会破坏唇同步与表情。

Method: 提出 DynaForcing 训练框架，在三个层面施加互补策略：(1) 数据层 Hybrid Forcing，将 rollout 锚定到真值动态以切断反馈回路；(2) 损失层 Dynamics-Aware Reward Regularization，借助 DMD 的强化学习解释引入显式运动奖励以抵消反向 KL 偏差；(3) 条件层 Reference Perturbation，扰动参考图像以解耦身份与静态细节，迫使模型依赖音频驱动运动。此外引入计算图剪枝与梯度回放，使 self-forcing 的显存占用降低一个数量级以上。

Result: 实验表明 DynaForcing 将动态恢复到与教师模型相当的水平（Dyn-Deg 从 0.31 提升至 0.73，Sync-C 从 7.03 提升至 7.68），同时提升视觉质量，在整个训练过程中解决了质量-动态之间的权衡，无需早停。

Conclusion: DynaForcing 通过数据层、损失层、条件层的三重策略及显存优化技术，系统性地解决了 DMD 自蒸馏中反向 KL 偏差与无锚自条件所导致的动态崩溃，在保持实时与视觉质量的同时恢复时间动态，消除了早期停止的必要性。

Abstract: Audio-driven avatar generation requires realistic lip-sync, expressive motion, and real-time streaming. Recent work achieves the latter via self-forcing with Distribution Matching Distillation (DMD), but this paradigm suffers from a critical failure that has not been systematically characterized: dynamic collapse, where the student model converges to a near-static optimum with high perceptual quality but severely suppressed temporal dynamics. We trace this to two causes: the reverse KL objective in DMD, which biases toward low-motion modes, and unanchored self-conditioning, which creates a feedback loop that amplifies collapse. This is especially harmful for avatars, where even subtle motion loss breaks lip-sync and expression.
  To address this, we propose DynaForcing, a training framework with three complementary strategies applied at different levels. Specifically, Hybrid Forcing anchors rollouts to ground-truth dynamics at the data level to break the feedback loop. Dynamics-Aware Reward Regularization introduces explicit motion rewards via the RL interpretation of DMD to counteract the reverse KL bias at the loss level. Reference Perturbation perturbs reference images to decouple identity from static details, forcing the model to rely on audio for motion at the conditioning level. We further introduce computation graph pruning and gradient replay, reducing the GPU footprint of self-forcing by over an order of magnitude. Experiments show that DynaForcing recovers dynamics to teacher-comparable levels (Dyn-Deg: 0.31 -> 0.73, Sync-C: 7.03 -> 7.68) while improving visual quality, resolving the quality-dynamics trade-off throughout training without early stopping.

</details>


### [64] [Vision-Language Models for Analog Gauge Reading: An Empirical Study of Specialization, Transfer and Reliability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17723)
*Abdul Mueez,Aaditya Baranwal,Junior Chaj-Mejia,Guneet Bhatia,Jason T. Voelker,Shruti Vyas*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了通用视觉语言模型 Qwen2.5-VL-7B-Instruct 直接读取单目标模拟仪表图像的能力，测试了零样本提示、上下文学习与 QLoRA 微调三种策略。微调后最佳范围归一化平均百分比误差在合成、压力表与工业数据集上分别为 2.39%、2.61% 和 4.43%。跨数据集迁移与鲁棒性仍不足，高置信度错误依然存在，表明该方法可支持单一仪表读取，但尚不足以直接部署到工厂监测流水线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模拟仪表在工业环境中仍普遍存在，但人工巡检成本高且具危险性。现有方法多依赖显式的指针分割与几何读数流水线，本文旨在不借助该流水线的情况下，系统评估通用视觉语言模型直接进行仪表数值读取的可行性，填补其在专业化（specialization）、迁移（transfer）、鲁棒性（robustness）与可靠性（reliability）方面的系统评测空白。

Method: 以 Qwen2.5-VL-7B-Instruct 为骨干，比较零样本提示、上下文学习（ICL）以及基于量化低秩适配（QLoRA）的参数高效微调三种策略。实验数据覆盖公开合成数据集、视频衍生的 Pressure Gauge 数据集与专有工业数据集。所有微调实验采用固定 20 轮协议并以最后一轮模型分析，同时分别训练提供/不提供仪表量程的模型以消除提示设置干扰。主要评价指标为范围归一化平均百分比误差（MPE），并给出 95% bootstrap 置信区间。

Result: 微调后最佳 MPE：合成数据集 2.39%（CI 1.43–3.90%），Pressure Gauge 数据集 2.61%（CI 1.66–3.80%），专有工业数据集 4.43%（CI 2.31–7.14%）。留一数据集实验显示，在未见过的合成与专有数据上迁移性能显著下降；鲁棒性测试表明高斯模糊是影响最强的扰动；可靠性分析发现高置信度下仍可能出现错误读数。

Conclusion: QLoRA 特化的视觉语言模型可有效支持单仪表直接读数，但尚未达到可直接部署于工厂监测流水线的成熟度。跨数据集泛化弱与高置信错误风险促使在安全关键场景中引入弃权（abstention）机制与独立验证。

Abstract: Analog gauges remain common in industrial environments where manual inspection is costly or hazardous. The engineering application addressed here is direct numerical reading of single-target analog-gauge images, while the artificial-intelligence contribution is a systematic evaluation of specialization, transfer, robustness and reliability for a general-purpose vision-language model (VLM) without an explicit pointer-segmentation and geometric-reading pipeline. The Qwen2.5-VL-7B-Instruct model is evaluated using zero-shot prompting, in-context learning (ICL) and parameter-efficient fine-tuning with Quantized Low-Rank Adaptation (QLoRA) on a public synthetic dataset, a video-derived Pressure Gauge dataset and a proprietary industrial dataset. All fine-tuning experiments use a fixed 20-epoch protocol with the final epoch used for analysis; separate models with and without supplied gauge ranges remove prompt-setting confounds. The primary metric is range-normalized mean percentage error (MPE). The best fine-tuned MPE values are 2.39% on the synthetic dataset, with a 95% bootstrap confidence interval (CI) of 1.43-3.90%; 2.61% on the Pressure Gauge dataset, with a CI of 1.66-3.80%; and 4.43% on the proprietary industrial dataset, with a CI of 2.31-7.14%. Leave-one-dataset-out experiments reveal substantial transfer degradation on held-out synthetic and proprietary data, while robustness tests identify Gaussian blur as the strongest tested corruption. Reliability analysis shows that high-confidence errors remain possible, motivating abstention and independent validation in safety-critical use. These results support QLoRA-specialized VLMs for direct single-gauge reading but not yet a deployment-ready plant-monitoring pipeline.

</details>


### [65] [Evaluation of AI-based Visual Crack Detection in Steel Bridges Using Probability of Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17726)
*Andrii Kompanets,Finn Michael Sherry,Remco Duits,Davide Leonetti,H. H. Snijder*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于检出概率（PoD）曲线的统计评估框架，用于将计算机视觉裂缝检测方法与常规人工目视检测进行对比，并在真实钢桥裂缝数据集上验证其有效性，证明自动化方法可作为常规检测的有力补充。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 桥梁结构需定期检测裂缝、腐蚀等损伤以保障公共安全并降低维护成本。虽然基于计算机视觉的自动化检测研究众多，但通常用IoU、mAP等指标评估，这些指标难以直接预测方法在结构工程实际中的有效性，因此亟需一种与标准工程方法兼容的评估方式来推动自动化方法在工程实践中的系统化应用。

Method: 提出一种新的统计评估框架，基于概率检测（Probability of Detection, PoD）曲线，用于比较计算机视觉方法与常规目视检测在钢桥裂缝检测中的表现，并能够考虑图像分辨率的影响。将该方法应用于真实数据集 "Cracks in Steel Bridges"（含带标注的桥梁结构裂缝图像）进行实证评估。

Result: 该框架能够量化检出概率及其不确定性，从而可在结构可靠性分析中对自动化损伤检测方法的效果做出实用评估。实证结果表明，所提出的计算机视觉方法在裂缝检测任务上具有鲁棒性，作为常规目视检测方法的补充可带来显著的高附加值。

Conclusion: 该评估方法为基于AI的自动化损伤检测在安全关键场景中的广泛应用奠定了基础，能够推动其在工程实践中的系统化采用，使计算机视觉裂缝检测方法成为常规目视检测的有效补充。

Abstract: Bridge structures are regularly inspected for structural damage such as cracks and corrosion in order to ensure public safety and reduce maintenance costs. Much research has been done on automating this process using computer vision methods, which are often evaluated and compared using metrics such as intersection over union, mean average precision, etc. However, predicting the actual effectiveness of an inspection method within the field of structural engineering from these metrics remains challenging. To enable the systematic use of these increasingly popular methods in engineering practice, evaluating the performance of these methods in a way that is compatible with standard engineering approaches is therefore an urgent necessity. We present a new statistical evaluation framework to allow the comparison of computer vision methods with conventional visual inspection for crack detection in steel bridges. The framework is based on probability of detection curves and can account for the influence of image resolution. We apply this evaluation method to the real-world ``Cracks in Steel Bridges'' dataset, which contains annotated images of cracks in bridge structures. The quantification of the probability of detection and its uncertainty enables a practical assessment of the effect of automated methods for damage detection in structural reliability analyses. In turn, this enables the wide-spread use of automated (AI-based) damage detection in safety critical applications. This evaluation method provides evidence that the proposed computer vision approach approach is robust for the crack detection task and can have a high added value as an addition to conventional visual inspection methods.

</details>


### [66] [TINA+: Probing Residual Visual Knowledge in Unlearned Diffusion Models via Diffusion-Consistent Text-Free Inversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17747)
*Qianlong Xiang,Miao Zhang,Kun Wang,Haoyu Zhang,Junhui Hou,Liqiang Nie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对概念擦除方法多停留在切断文本-图像映射、却可能残留视觉知识的问题，本文提出一种无文本的扩散反演攻击 TINA+，通过扩散一致轨迹正则化抑制伪轨迹，从而可靠地探测擦除概念是否仍能在视觉层面被重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有概念擦除与对抗探测方法高度以文本为中心，只关注文本到图像映射是否被切断，忽视了底层视觉知识是否仍被模型保留。作者希望从纯视觉角度回答：被擦除概念的视觉实例能否仅凭生成轨迹被重建。

Method: 提出 TINA+，一种扩散一致的文本无关反演攻击，采用基于优化的反演，并引入 Diffusion-Consistent Trajectory Regularization。该正则项通过惩罚远低于扩散过程边际能量期望演化轨迹的反演路径，抑制无约束反演可能发现的伪轨迹（这些伪轨迹甚至能让随机初始化的扩散模型重建目标概念），从而避免对残余视觉知识的误判。

Result: 在十二种擦除方法、四个概念擦除任务及不同模型架构上的实验表明，TINA+ 能通过扩散一致的视觉轨迹可靠探测残余视觉知识，且发现当前方法往往是通过切断文本-图像联系来掩盖概念，而非真正消除底层视觉知识。

Conclusion: TINA+ 为概念擦除提供了更强的视觉层面评估证据，证明现有擦除方法在很大程度上只是遮蔽文本映射而非根除视觉知识，提示未来擦除技术应同时处理底层视觉知识。

Abstract: Although text-to-image diffusion models exhibit remarkable generative power, concept erasure techniques are essential for preventing harmful content. Existing adversarial probes evaluate these methods by testing whether erased concepts can still be recovered. However, existing erasure and probe methods remain largely text-centric, focusing on whether the text-to-image mapping is severed while overlooking whether the corresponding visual knowledge remains. To investigate this question from a visual perspective, we leverage diffusion inversion to probe whether a generative trajectory can reconstruct visual instances of an erased concept. Under a null-text condition, standard inversion avoids the textual pathway but amplifies approximation errors, hindering faithful trajectory recovery. To address this challenge, we introduce TINA+, a diffusion-consistent Text-free INversion Attack equipped with optimization-based inversion. We also find that unconstrained diffusion inversion may discover spurious trajectories, even allowing a randomly initialized diffusion model to reconstruct the target concept. Such trajectories may falsely indicate residual visual knowledge. TINA+ therefore introduces Diffusion-Consistent Trajectory Regularization to suppress this failure mode. By penalizing trajectories that fall far below the expected marginal energy evolution of diffusion, TINA+ suppresses spurious inversion paths while preserving its ability to recover erased concepts. Experiments across twelve erasure methods, four concept-erasure tasks, and different model architectures demonstrate that TINA+ reliably probes residual visual knowledge through diffusion-consistent visual trajectories. These results provide stronger evidence that current methods often obscure concepts by severing text-image links rather than eliminating the underlying visual knowledge.

</details>


### [67] [Training with synthetic data for drone detection in thermal imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17799)
*Tanel Liiv,Sander Soodla,Nzamba Bignoumba,Alma M. Liezenga,Toomas Pruuden*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究可见光/长波红外（MWIR/LWIR）图像中地对空（G2A）无人机检测难题，提出“合成数据优先训练+真实数据微调”策略，发现合成数据可学习初始目标表征，少量真实红外数据即可显著缩小域差异，且数据集对齐比模型规模更重要，特征空间语义对齐是性能最强预测因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地对空无人机红外检测面临纹理信息少、传感器噪声、热对比度弱以及标注数据稀缺等挑战，需要探索在不充分真实数据条件下提升检测性能的有效训练策略。

Method: 采用“合成优先”（synthetic-first）训练策略：利用合成场景生成数据学习初始物体表征，再在真实数据上微调；并通过实验对比真实数据量、数据集对齐程度与模型规模对性能的影响，分析语义对齐及熵、动态范围等辐射特性对检测稳健性的贡献。

Result: 合成数据为初始目标表征学习提供了有效基础，但真实域内热红外图像仍不可或缺；即使少量真实红外数据也能显著减小域差异；数据集对齐对性能的影响强于模型规模；特征空间语义对齐是模型性能的最强预测因子，辐射属性（熵、动态范围）也影响检测稳健性。

Conclusion: 本研究为结合合成与真实红外数据进行有效地对空无人机检测奠定了基础，表明“合成预训练+真实微调”是实现低成本、可靠部署的可行路径。

Abstract: Ground-to-Air (G2A) drone detection in medium- and long-wave infrared (MWIR/LWIR) imagery is challenging due to reduced texture information, sensor noise, weak thermal contrast, and the scarcity of annotated data. This work investigates a synthetic-first training strategy that combines synthetic scene generation with fine-tuning on real data. We show that synthetic data provides an effective basis for learning initial object representations, while real in-domain thermal imagery is still essential for reliable deployment. Even small amounts of real IR data substantially reduce domain gaps. Our experiments indicate that dataset alignment has a stronger impact on performance than model scale. Finally, our analysis of the dataset suggests that semantic alignment in feature space is the strongest predictor of model performance, while radiometric properties such as entropy and dynamic range also contribute to detection robustness. This work provides a foundation for combining synthetic and real IR data for effective G2A drone detection.

</details>


### [68] [Scale Matters: Adaptive Granularity Selection for Cross-Species 3D Plant Organ Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17803)
*Carla Salazar,Lazaros Nalpantidis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 AGS-PlantSeg，一种基于冻结 3D 基础模型 + 自适应粒度选择的少样本 3D 植物器官分割方法，在多个植物数据集上以极少标注取得 88.9% 平均 mIoU，优于固定粒度基线 2.5 个点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 3D 基础模型依赖固定的空间粒度控制，难以适应植物表型分析中不同物种、不同生长阶段器官形态与尺寸的高度差异，导致跨物种泛化能力受限。

Method: 将冻结的 Utonia 3D 基础模型与自适应粒度选择（Adaptive Granularity Selection）相结合，针对每种具体植物动态挑选最优粒度层级，提取优化的几何特征，并送入一个轻量级 MLP 分割头完成少样本分割。

Result: 在 PLANesT-3D、Pheno4D 和 Crops3D 上大量实验表明，AGS-PlantSeg 显著提升跨物种泛化，平均 mIoU 达 88.9%，比固定粒度基线高 2.5 个 mIoU 点；在仅需极少量标注数据的情况下，性能可与植物专用全监督架构相媲美。

Conclusion: 自适应粒度选择能有效克服固定空间粒度的局限，以极小标注成本实现与全监督、植物专用方法相当的分割精度，验证了该少样本方法在植物器官分割中的有效性与实用性。

Abstract: Recent 3D foundation models provide powerful feature representations for point cloud learning by controlling spatial granularity. However, relying on a fixed spatial granularity severely limits generalization in applications like plant phenotyping, where organ morphology and size vary substantially across species and growth stages. To address this, we propose AGS-PlantSeg, a few-shot 3D plant organ segmentation method that leverages the frozen Utonia (arXiv:2603.03283) foundation model combined with Adaptive Granularity Selection. By dynamically selecting the best granularity levels for each specific plant model, our method extracts optimized geometric features for a lightweight MLP segmentation head. Extensive experiments across PLANesT-3D (arXiv:2407.21150), Pheno4D , and Crops3D demonstrate that AGS-PlantSeg significantly improves cross-species generalization, achieving 88.9% average mIoU performance and outperforming fixed-granularity baselines by 2.5 mIoU points. Despite requiring minimal annotated data, our approach is highly competitive with fully supervised, plant-specific architectures.

</details>


### [69] [GenRec: Knowing Where to Reconstruct and Where to Generate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17832)
*Ata Çelen,Jaewoo Jung,Federico Tombari,Marc Pollefeys,Sunghwan Hong,Michael Niemeyer,Daniel Barath*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GenRec 提出一种多视点流匹配模型，将"重建"与"生成"两种机制在架构、监督与梯度流层面显式分离，在稀疏输入下的新视角合成中兼顾可观测区域的高保真重建与不可见区域的合理生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式新视角合成方法用统一的损失函数混合处理两类本质不同的像素：源视图中可见的像素有唯一正确值（仅受视角相关着色影响），而被遮挡或超出采集范围的像素则存在合理补全的分布。这种混合模糊了几何保真度与创造性幻觉的界限，即便通过变形点云或投影深度注入场景几何也无法解决。

Method: GenRec 是一个多视点流匹配模型，将重建-生成的分割直接构建进架构、监督和梯度流中。具体包括：1) 基于源相机与单目深度估计器推导观测掩码；2) 流匹配骨干网络在所有目标视点上联合去噪 RGB 与场景坐标图；3) 像素级细化阶段恢复可观测像素上的高频细节；4) 该掩码同时门控监督信号，防止回归信号污染生成先验。

Result: 在 RealEstate10K、DL3DV-10K 和 Mip-NeRF 360 三个数据集上，针对单视点外推和双视点插值任务，GenRec 在可观测区域取得最佳重建保真度，同时在不可见区域的感知质量上超越纯生成式基线。

Conclusion: 通过在模型架构和训练中显式区分重建与生成两种机制，GenRec 有效解决了稀疏输入新视角合成中几何保真与生成补全相互干扰的问题，验证了该方法的有效性。

Abstract: Generative novel view synthesis from sparse input images is rarely all reconstruction or all generation: pixels visible in some source view have a unique correct value modulated only by view-dependent shading, while pixels in disocclusions or beyond the captured volume admit a distribution of plausible completions. Existing generative novel-view-synthesis methods conflate these regimes under a single uniform loss, blurring the line between geometric fidelity and creative hallucinations even when scene geometry is injected through warped point clouds or projected depth. We introduce GenRec, a multi-view flow matching model that builds the reconstruction--generation split directly into its architecture, supervision, and gradient flow. Guided by an observation mask derived from the source cameras and a monocular depth estimator, a flow matching backbone jointly denoises RGB and scene-coordinate maps across all target views, while a pixel-space refinement stage restores high-frequency detail on observed pixels; the same mask gates supervision so regression signals do not contaminate the generative prior. Across RealEstate10K, DL3DV-10K, and Mip-NeRF~360, in both single-view extrapolation and two-view interpolation, GenRec attains the best reconstruction fidelity in observed regions while also surpassing purely generative baselines on perceptual quality in unobserved ones, showing the effectiveness of our approach.

</details>


### [70] [DistillPath: An Efficient 22M Distilled Pathology Encoder Approaching Large Foundation Model Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17872)
*Ramon Kaspar,Andrey Ignatov,Valentina Boeva*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 DistillPath-KS16，一种紧凑型病理切片编码器：以 22M 参数的 kaiko ViT-S/16 为学生模型，通过仅使用冻结教师模型暴露的 class/patch token 进行知识蒸馏，在 6,000 张公开切片上训练，将 86M 至 1.1B 参数的四个病理基础模型蒸馏到同一小模型。结果在 EVA、HEST、PLISM 三个基准上均优于 kaiko 基线，最强变体在七任务 EVA 均值上接近 Virchow2，参数减少约 29 倍、推理快 25 倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前高性能病理切片编码器多为参数量达数亿至十亿以上的基础模型，在普通硬件上对整张切片中数千个 tile 进行编码与存储成本高昂，因此需要能保持良好下游性能的紧凑型编码器作为替代。

Method: 以已有的 22M 参数 kaiko ViT-S/16 编码器为学生模型，从已发布的病理编码器（作为冻结教师）进行蒸馏。该方法仅读取教师模型最终的 class token 与 patch token，在 6,000 张公开切片上训练，无需教师的 DINO 或 iBOT 预训练头，也无需十亿级 tile 语料，因此适用于任何暴露 backbone token 的已发布编码器。实验中蒸馏了 4 个参数量从 86M 到 1.1B 的教师模型到同一学生模型。

Result: 每个蒸馏变体在 EVA、HEST、PLISM 三个基准上均优于 kaiko 基线，且最强教师取决于具体任务。在七任务 EVA 均值上，DistillPath-KS16-Virchow2 达到 0.795，与本评估中得分最高的 Virchow2 仅差 0.015 分，而参数量减少约 29 倍；该变体在此聚合指标上还高于 H0-mini 和 GPFM，但优势集中在特定任务而非均匀分布。由于仍是具有 384 维特征的 22M ViT-S/16，其运行速度比 Virchow2 快 25 倍以上。

Conclusion: DistillPath-KS16 证明仅用教师模型的 backbone token 和少量公开切片即可将大病理基础模型有效蒸馏为 22M 的紧凑模型，在保持接近大模型下游性能的同时大幅降低计算与存储成本，并已开源代码与模型权重。

Abstract: Many high-performing pathology tile encoders are now foundation models with hundreds of millions to over a billion parameters. Encoding and storing the thousands of tiles in each whole-slide image with such models is costly on commodity hardware, so compact encoders that retain useful downstream performance are a valuable alternative. We present DistillPath-KS16, which starts from the existing 22M kaiko ViT-S/16 encoder and improves it by distilling from released pathology encoders used as frozen teachers. The recipe reads only the teachers' final class and patch tokens and trains on 6,000 public slides, needing neither their DINO nor iBOT pretraining heads nor a billion-tile corpus, so it applies to any released encoder that exposes backbone tokens. We distill four teachers spanning 86M to 1.1B parameters into the same student. Every variant improves the kaiko baseline on all three benchmarks we use, EVA, HEST, and PLISM, and the strongest teacher is task-dependent. On the seven-task EVA mean, DistillPath-KS16-Virchow2 reaches $0.795$, within $0.015$ points of Virchow2, the top-scoring model in our evaluation, at about $29\times$ fewer parameters; it also scores above H0-mini and GPFM on this aggregate metric, though that advantage is task-concentrated rather than uniform. Because it remains a 22M ViT-S/16 with 384-dimensional features, DistillPath-KS16 runs more than $25\times$ faster than Virchow2. Code is available at https://github.com/RamonKaspar/DistillPath, and released model weights are available at https://huggingface.co/collections/RamonK/distillpath.

</details>


### [71] [Improving Complex Moiré Removal with Generative Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17883)
*Xinyang Gu,Zhilu Zhang,Honglei Xu,Yanting Mei,Yukang Ding,Wangmeng Zuo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出一个数据引擎，利用多个图像条件生成基础模型为真实世界复杂摩尔纹图像生成干净参考图，经分块级质量控制筛选后构建 WildMoiré 数据集（6.8K 训练对），并验证其监督能一致提升复杂摩尔纹去除性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习型图像去摩尔纹模型依赖高质量配对数据，但现有数据集难以涵盖真实无控场景下的大尺度、多色彩复杂摩尔纹；这类图像的干净对应物（如来自公共显示屏或网络资源的照片）难以获取，导致训练监督不足。

Method: 提出一个系统化数据引擎：(1) 收集含复杂摩尔纹的真实图像并定位屏幕区域；(2) 部署多个图像条件生成基础模型生成候选参考图；(3) 对候选进行分块级质量控制，筛选并选择最优结果作为监督；(4) 据此构建 WildMoiré 数据集（6.8K 摩尔纹-GT 训练对），并另建约 250 对含真实干净真值的独立测试集进行评估。

Result: 在 ESDNet、SDXL 和 Qwen-Image-Edit 上的大量实验表明，所提出的生成式监督一致提升了复杂摩尔纹去除的性能。

Conclusion: 通过生成基础模型与分块级质量控制构建的训练监督，能有效缓解复杂摩尔纹配对数据稀缺问题，并为真实场景复杂摩尔纹去除提供可靠的训练数据与评测基准。

Abstract: The availability of high-quality paired data is essential for training learning-based image demoiréing models. However, it remains challenging for existing datasets to encompass the complex moiré patterns captured in uncontrolled real-world scenarios. Such degradations typically manifest as large-scale, multicolored moiré patterns. Moreover, these patterns frequently occur in images for which clean counterparts are difficult to obtain, such as photographs acquired from public displays or existing online resources. In this work, we propose a novel data engine designed to improve the removal of complex moiré patterns by generating training supervision. Specifically, we initially collect real-world images containing complex moiré patterns and localize the corresponding screen regions. Multiple image-conditioned generative foundation models are subsequently deployed to produce candidate references. To establish reliable supervision, these candidates are subjected to patch-level quality control to filter and select the optimal results. Based on this systematic paradigm, we construct the WildMoiré dataset, which contains 6.8K moiré-GT training pairs. For evaluation, we additionally build an independent test set comprising $\sim$250 pairs with captured clean ground truth. Extensive experiments on ESDNet, SDXL, and Qwen-Image-Edit demonstrate that the proposed generative supervision consistently improves the performance of complex moiré removal.

</details>


### [72] [CFB-GBM v2.0: An Augmented Longitudinal Dataset for Multi-Modal Glioblastoma Segmentation, Radiomics, and RANO Progression Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17884)
*Alexandre G. Leclercq,Noémie N. Moreau,Hugo Audebert,Andros Nassar,Thomas Cochin,Thomas Leleu,Loïc Le Henaff,Alexis Desmonts,Yoann Poirier,Aurélie Dubru,Laura Guillemette,Pascal Lecoeur,Kévin Lemasson,Cyril Jaudet,Sébastien Bougleux,Romain Hérault,Carole Brunaud,Samuel Valable,Dinu Stefan,Charlotte Raboutet,Alain Batalla,Joëlle Lacroix,Roman Rouzier,Aurélien Corroyer-Dulmont*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍CFB-GBM v2.0数据集，它是CFB-GBM的扩展版本，通过深度学习方法补全了264例胶质母细胞瘤患者在所有时间点的GTV标注（完成率从35%提升至97%），并据此派生RANO 2.0纵向反应类别标签及预计算放射组学特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 胶质母细胞瘤(GBM)是成人最具侵袭性的原发性脑肿瘤，中位总生存期仅15个月。为支持治疗反应预测、疾病进展建模和个体化医疗的可复现计算方法开发，亟需具备全面临床与治疗数据的纵向、多模态影像数据集，且需要完整的时间序列肿瘤标注。

Method: 作者在先前发布的CFB-GBM数据集基础上扩展。使用在BraTS 2021上预训练、并在CFB-GBM真实轮廓上微调的nnU-Net模型，自动分割Gross Tumour Volume(GTV)，补全所有可用时间点(t0、t1、t2)的标注，并经5位放射肿瘤科医生验证。随后从纵向GTV标注派生RANO 2.0体积响应类别标签；使用HD-BET计算脑掩膜，并用PyRadiomics提取预计算放射组学特征；同时明确记录每位患者适用的WHO分类指南(2016版或2021版)。

Result: GTV总体完成率从35%提升至97%；为所有可用时间对(t0→t1、t0→t2、t1→t2)生成了体积RANO 2.0响应类别标签；并为每位患者时间点和MRI模态提供脑掩膜及预计算放射组学特征；WHO分类指南版本(2016 vs. 2021)已被明确记录。数据集已公开于TCIA。

Conclusion: CFB-GBM v2.0通过补全纵向GTV标注并附加RANO 2.0标签、脑掩膜和放射组学特征，显著提升了数据集完整性与可用性，为治疗反应预测、疾病进展建模和个体化医疗提供了更完善的可复现计算资源，并已公开可获取。

Abstract: Glioblastoma (GBM) is the most aggressive primary brain tumor in adults, with a median overall survival of 15 months. Longitudinal, multi-modal imaging datasets with comprehensive clinical and treatment data are essential to support the development of reproducible computational methods for treatment response prediction, disease progression modelling, and personalized medicine. We present CFB-GBM v2.0, an extension of our previously released CFB-GBM dataset comprising 264 GBM patients treated according to the standard Stupp protocol. The primary contribution of this release is the completion of Gross Tumour Volume (GTV) delineations across all available timepoints ($t_0$, $t_1$ and $t_2$), increasing the overall GTV completion rate from 35% to 97%. This was achieved using a nnU-Net model pre-trained on BraTS 2021 and fine-tuned on CFB-GBM ground-truth contours, with the generated segmentations validated by five radiation oncologists. From these longitudinal GTV annotations, volumetric RANO 2.0 response category labels were derived for all available temporality pairs ($t_0 \rightarrow t_1$, $t_0 \rightarrow t_2$ and $t_1 \rightarrow t_2$). To further ease dataset usability and reproducibility, brain masks computed with HD-BET and pre-computed radiomic features extracted with PyRadiomics are provided for each patient timepoint and MRI modality. Additionally, the WHO classification guideline (2016 vs. 2021) applicable to each patient's diagnosis is now explicitly documented. CFB-GBM v2.0 is publicly available on The Cancer Imaging Archive (TCIA) at https://www.cancerimagingarchive.net/collection/cfb-gbm .

</details>


### [73] [Comparative Study of Out-of-the-Box Technology for Automatic Target Detection and Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17917)
*Alma M. Liezenga,Lotte Nijskens,Henrik R. Baumann,Stefan Becker,Simon Bensberg,Niccolò Camarlinghi,Håvard R. Eiring,Alexander W. Johnsgaard,Tanel Liiv,Giuseppe Martino,Matteo Marturini,Matthias Rapp,Jan Erik van Woerden,Alexander Wolpert,Hugo J. Kuijf*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文在公开的军事相关数据集上对多种先进目标检测模型（YOLO系列六代及DETR两种变体）进行基准测试，并将模型在VisDrone民航小目标数据集上微调以考察领域迁移能力，评估其在军事自动目标检测与识别（ATD/R）任务中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 军事ATD/R对决策支持与（半）自主作战至关重要，但公开军事数据集匮乏限制了其应用。作者希望探究能否借助公开模型与民用数据集（如VisDrone）在军事场景下取得合理性能。

Method: 在一个新采集的、包含军事车辆及遮挡、小目标等挑战的军事数据集上，对六代YOLO系列和两种DETR框架模型进行基准测试。每个模型均比较开箱即用版本与在VisDrone（小目标、空对地视角）上微调后的版本，并采用mAP@0.5和mAP@0.5:0.95指标，从空对地（A2G）与地对地（G2G）视角、目标尺寸及模型规模等维度对比评估。

Result: 主要发现：(1) 更大的模型优于更小的模型；(2) 基于DETR的模型相较YOLO系列表现有前景；(3) 在域外A2G数据集上微调可提升模型的A2G性能，并对小目标检测略有改善；(4) 所有模型在A2G场景下检测小目标仍存在困难。

Conclusion: 尽管目标检测技术近年进步显著，域内训练（in-domain training）对于构建高性能ATD/R系统仍然至关重要。

Abstract: Automatic Target Detection and Recognition (ATD/R) is critical for military decision support and (semi-)autonomous operations. Recent advances in object detection and artificial intelligence (AI) significantly boosted the potential performance of ATD/R. However, the scarcity of publicly available military datasets limits the application of these systems. As a solution, this paper explores the use of publicly available models and civilian datasets to achieve reasonable performance in military contexts. We benchmark several state-of-the-art models, including six iterations of the YOLO series and two variations on the DETR framework, on a newly acquired military relevant dataset. This dataset features military vehicles and challenging circumstances, including various degrees of occlusions and small targets. The out-of-the-box version of each model is validated alongside a version finetuned on the VisDrone dataset. This dataset features small objects, an Air-to-Ground (A2G) perspective and relevant classes, potentially generalizing to our military ATD/R task. We compare the performance of the models using mAP@0.5 and mAP@0.5:0.95, across A2G and Ground-to-Ground (G2G) perspective, target size and model size, giving insight into the real-time capabilities of models. Our main findings are: (1) bigger models outperform smaller models, (2) DETR-based models show promising results compared to the YOLO series,(3) fine-tuning models on an out-of-domain A2G dataset, improves their A2G performance and slightly improves their performance on small objects, but (4) all models still struggle with detecting small objects in an A2G scenario. We conclude that, despite recent advances in object detection, in-domain training is still crucial for creating capable ATD/R systems.

</details>


### [74] [AppendiGrade: An XAI-Enhanced Deep Learning Framework for Grading Appendicitis in Ultrasound with Gaussian Blur and Grad-CAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17923)
*Fahad Ahammed,Omar Faruq Shikdar,Navid Zaman,Md Tahsin,Md. Nawab Yousuf Ali,Golam Sorwar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文开发了一种基于深度学习的系统，利用超声图像自动检测复杂性阑尾炎（穿孔、脓肿等），通过对InceptionV3等预训练模型进行优化，最终将分类准确率从69.21%提升至95.58%，并用Grad-CAM解释模型预测区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阑尾炎是全球最常见的腹部急症之一，需及时诊断治疗以避免危及生命。然而，准确区分复杂性阑尾炎（如穿孔或脓肿形成）与单纯性阑尾炎在临床上仍是一大难题。超声因无辐射、更安全且成本效益更高，是较优的诊断手段，因此需要开发能自动检测复杂性阑尾炎的智能系统。

Method: 构建了包含4679张超声图像、共5个类别（穿孔、脓肿、急性、阑尾粪石、正常）的数据集。采用四个预训练深度学习模型（DenseNet201、InceptionV3、ConvNextTiny、VGG19）进行检测与分类。针对原始图像的次优性能，进一步应用图像预处理、超参数调优、模型微调和图像锐化等优化技术。利用梯度加权类激活映射（Grad-CAM）生成热力图，解释模型预测所依赖的感染区域。

Result: 在初始配置下，InceptionV3取得了第二高的准确率，为69.21%。经过优化技术处理后，模型性能显著提升，InceptionV3的准确率达到95.58%。Grad-CAM热力图能够定位模型用于预测的感染区域，便于与专家进行交叉核对。

Conclusion: 通过图像预处理、超参数调优、微调和图像锐化等优化手段，基于InceptionV3的深度学习系统能够以95.58%的准确率自动区分复杂性阑尾炎，并结合Grad-CAM提供可解释性，为临床辅助诊断提供了有效且成本较低的方案。

Abstract: Appendicitis is one of the most common abdominal emergencies worldwide and requires prompt diagnosis and treatment to prevent life-threatening conditions. However, accurately differentiating complicated cases, such as perforation or abscess formation, from uncomplicated appendicitis remains a significant clinical challenge. Among other methods, ultrasound is a safer and more cost-efficient diagnostic technique because of the lack of radiation exposure. In this research, an advanced system capable of automatically detecting complicated appendicitis from ultrasound images was developed. A dataset consisting of 4679 ultrasound images with 5 classes, namely perforated, abscess, acute, appendicolith, and normal, was used for the proposed model training and testing. Four pretrained deep learning models, DenseNet201, InceptionV3, ConvNextTiny, and VGG19, have been employed for detecting and classifying complicated appendicitis. In the initial configuration, InceptionV3 achieved the second highest accuracy, with a value of 69.21%. Owing to suboptimal performance with raw images, further optimization techniques, including image preprocessing, hyperparameter tuning, model fine-tuning, and image sharpening, were applied. These enhancements significantly improved the model's performance, with an accuracy of 95.58% for InceptionV3. The model performance is then explained with gradient-weighted class activation mapping (Grad-CAM), which creates a heatmap of the regions responsible for the model's prediction of the infected areas. This could make crosschecking with experts much easier.

</details>


### [75] [PerFact: Perception-Derived Fact Prompting for 3D Brain MRI Report Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17926)
*Jianyu Sun,Zhenxuan Zhang,Guang Yang,Peter J. Lally*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文挑战了"更大/更强骨干模型是提升放射报告质量的关键"这一默认假设，在3D多序列脑MRI这一体积多疾病场景中发现：真正决定报告质量的是注入提示中的"接地信息"（grounding），而非模型选择。作者提出PerFact方法，将上游3D分割与分类的结果序列化为结构化事实句来提示LoRA适配的视觉语言模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有放射报告生成研究几乎完全建立在2D胸部X光片上，默认通过更大的骨干网络或在医学数据上的预训练来改进报告质量。作者质疑这一假设在3D多序列脑MRI这一体积多疾病场景中是否成立，发现零样本医学/放射学视觉语言模型迁移到脑MRI效果差（胸部X光专家表现最差），且不同规模、不同家族的五个骨干模型在相同微调下差异甚微。因此需要探究真正可控的决定性因素。

Method: 提出PerFact：将感知任务委托给上游的3D分割与分类模型，把其输出序列化为结构化的"事实句"（fact sentence），并用该事实句提示一个经LoRA适配的视觉语言模型来生成报告。在受控研究中固定骨干、数据划分、目标报告和适配方式，仅改变注入的接地信息进行对照。

Result: 感知派生的事实优于检索到的历史报告；一旦事实存在，检索便成为冗余；端到端预测的事实即使推理时无任何真实标注依然有效。预测事实与oracle事实之间的残差差距源于事实的粒度而非生成器本身。封闭式视觉问答的加入对报告质量无可测损失，且接地来源对问答影响甚微。

Conclusion: 在3D脑MRI场景下，接地信息（而非模型选择）是报告质量中占主导地位的可控因素。未来改进报告生成应聚焦于注入提示的感知事实的质量与粒度，而非一味追求更大的骨干模型。

Abstract: Radiology report generation has matured almost entirely on 2D chest radiographs, where the default route to better reports is a larger backbone or a pre-training one on medical data. We revisit that assumption on 3D multi-sequence brain MRI, a volumetric multi-disease regime, and find that the model is not the lever. Zero-shot medical and radiology vision-language models transfer poorly to brain MRI, with chest radiograph specialists failing most conspicuously, and five backbones fine-tuned identically across three model families and an order of magnitude in scale differ only marginally. What determines the quality of the report is the information injected into the prompt. We delegate perception to upstream 3D segmentation and classification, serialize their outputs into a structured fact sentence, and prompt a LoRA-adapted vision-language model with it; we call this \textbf{PerFact}. In a controlled study that fixes the backbone, data split, target reports, and adaptation while varying only the injected grounding, perception-derived facts outperform retrieved prior reports, retrieval becomes redundant once facts are present, and end-to-end predicted facts remain effective without any ground-truth annotation at inference. The residual gap between predicted and oracle facts is explained by the granularity of the facts rather than by the generator. Closed-ended visual question answering comes at no measurable cost to report quality, though the grounding source has little effect on it. On 3D brain MRI, grounding information, not model choice, is the dominant controllable factor in report quality.

</details>


### [76] [Beyond Instrument Motion: Recognizing Tissue Tension Toward Surgical Skill Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17935)
*Marko Haralovi,Zhiqi Miao,Alexander Machiel Bont,Jiapan Guo,Frans van Workum,Estefania Talavera*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出组织张力识别这一新的外科视频理解任务，构建首个专家标注的 SurgTension 数据集，并设计轻量级轨迹框架 TensionTRAC，以稀疏点轨迹建模组织张力，性能媲美强预训练视频骨干网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微创手术的质量评估目前主要依赖人工专家评审，耗时、主观且难以规模化。现有手术视频理解方法聚焦于器械分割、阶段识别和动作识别，未显式捕捉细粒度的组织处理（tissue handling）——这是手术质量的关键指标。因此作者针对腹腔镜与机器人辅助直肠癌手术提出组织张力识别任务。

Method: 作者提出 TensionTRAC，一个基于轨迹的轻量级框架，通过稀疏点轨迹（sparse point trajectories）建模组织张力，并使用紧凑的轨迹编码器（compact trajectory encoder）进行特征提取与识别。

Result: TensionTRAC 在自建的 SurgTension 数据集上取得了竞争性性能，能够与强大的预训练视频骨干网络（pretrained video backbones）相媲美。

Conclusion: 组织张力识别填补了细粒度组织处理理解的空白，SurgTension 为客观的组织张力识别提供了基准，TensionTRAC 证明了以稀疏轨迹进行轻量级建模的可行性与有效性。

Abstract: Surgical performance assessment in minimally invasive surgery largely relies on manual expert review, making it time-consuming, subjective, and difficult to scale. While existing surgical video understanding methods address tasks such as instrument segmentation, surgical phase recognition, and action recognition, they do not explicitly capture fine-grained tissue handling, a key indicator of surgical quality. To address this gap, we introduce tissue tension recognition, a new clinically motivated video understanding task for laparoscopic and robot-assisted rectal cancer surgery. To support this task, we construct SurgTension, the first expert-annotated tissue tension dataset, providing a benchmark for objective tissue tension recognition. We further propose TensionTRAC, a lightweight trajectory-based framework that models tissue tension from sparse point trajectories. Using a compact trajectory encoder, TensionTRAC achieves competitive performance against strong pretrained video backbones.

</details>


### [77] [Cross-Domain Generalization in Machine Unlearning via Label-Conditioned Energy Magnitude Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17942)
*Syed Ali Ahmed,Syed Bilal Ahsan,Muhammad Zaigham Zaheer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究机器遗忘中"遗忘一个类别对模型其余部分的影响"，提出一种基于标签条件能量模型(EBM)的类别遗忘方法，通过提高目标类图像-标签对的能量来遗忘该类，并引入传播项使遗忘信号根据DINOv2相似度扩散到相似样本。实验表明，跨域遗忘可从素描域扩散到真实、剪贴画和绘画域，遗忘误差高达98%~99%；在CIFAR-10上逐类遗忘可达100%，其余九类平均保留98.5%的预遗忘精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大多数机器遗忘方法将被遗忘的概念视为孤立对象，忽略了遗忘一个概念后对模型其余部分产生的连锁影响。作者希望研究"遗忘一个类别时模型其余部分会发生什么"，并需要一个能直接观测每个类别能量变化的模型来刻画这种影响。

Method: 采用标签条件能量模型(EBM)，为每个类别分配能量，使遗忘效果可直接观测。遗忘某一类别时提高其图像-标签对的能量，训练目标包含：遗忘项(forget term)、对预训练模型的保留锚(retain anchor)、全局间隔(global margin)以及防止能量无限增长的能量正则项(energy regularizer)。核心创新是传播项(propagation term)，将该类别相同的遗忘信号按每个保留样本与遗忘类的DINOv2相似度加权后施加到保留样本上，使遗忘扩散到与被遗忘类相似的图像，同时保持其余部分不受影响。

Result: 在两个基准数据集上验证：1) 在DomainNet子集(四个视觉域)上逐一遗忘tiger、lion、scissors，在素描域遗忘一个类会同时从真实、剪贴画、绘画域中抹除该类，lion和scissors的遗忘误差达到98%和99%，且该效果会传播到最相似的类别；2) 在CIFAR-10上关闭传播项，逐一遗忘十个类，遗忘达到100%彻底，其余九个类平均保留98.5%的预遗忘精度。

Conclusion: 表明遗忘一个类别并非孤立事件，其影响会依据样本与遗忘类的相似性传播到模型的其他部分；所提出的基于能量模型加传播项的类别遗忘方法能够实现跨域、彻底的类别遗忘，同时最大程度保留其他类别的原始性能。

Abstract: Machine unlearning removes the influence of specific data from a trained model. However, most methods treat the forgotten concept as isolated. In this paper, we study what happens to the rest of the model when a class is forgotten, using a label-conditioned energy-based model (EBM) that assigns per-class energies, making the effect directly observable. We forget a class by raising the energy of its image-label pairs, training with a forget term, a retain anchor to the pretrained model, a global margin, and an energy regularizer that stops the energy magnitudes from growing without limit. A propagation term applies the same forget signal to retain samples, weighted by each sample's DINOv2 similarity to the forget class, so forgetting reaches images that resemble it and leaves the rest untouched. We evaluate on two benchmark datasets: 1) On a subset of DomainNet across four visual domains, we forget tiger, lion, and scissors one at a time. Forgetting a class in the sketch domain also erases it from real, clipart, and painting, with forgetting error reaching 98% and 99% for lion and scissors, and the effect carrying over to the most similar class. 2) On CIFAR-10, we turn off the propagation term and forget each of the ten classes on its own. Forgetting is complete (100%), while the other nine classes retain 98.5% of their pre-unlearning accuracy on average.

</details>


### [78] [SFMformer: A Spatial-Frequency Modulation Transformer for Lightweight Image Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17966)
*Chih-Hsiang Yang,Chia-Min Lin,Ching-Yu Tsai,Yung-Che Wang,Jen-Shiun Chiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 SFMformer，通过将稀疏注意力中的"选择质量"与"聚合质量"视为两个可分离的优化目标，分别用前置的双分支空间增强和后置的小波域调制来改善，在轻量级图像超分辨率任务上以不足百万参数取得领先性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏注意力机制（top-k 选择 + 传播最强 token）改变了"改进网络"的含义：稠密注意力只需关注聚合质量，而稀疏注意力因 top-k 算子先决定 token 存活再决定如何处理，一旦 token 在选择阶段被丢弃便无法在下游恢复。因此选择质量与聚合质量是两个可分离的优化目标，需由注意力前后不同位置的模块分别处理。

Method: 作者构建 SFMformer：在渐进聚焦注意力（progressive focused attention）的输入侧加入双分支空间增强（dual-branch spatial enhancement）以提升选择质量，在其输出侧加入小波域调制（wavelet-domain modulation）以提升聚合质量。通过在所有 15 个基准-尺度组合上单独与联合测量各模块，验证增益的可叠加性；并将谱调制改为每块一次（而非每层一次）以降低计算开销，使模型在各尺度均控制在百万参数以内。同时部署至 Raspberry Pi 5 验证资源受限下的实用性。

Result: 两个模块的增益并非简单相加：在 9 个基准-尺度对上联合增益超过各自单独增益之和；增益偏差的符号可由较弱模块单独贡献的大小预测（相关系数 r = -0.72），说明当两模块缓解不同约束时增益复合、缓解相同约束时增益重叠。每块一次（而非每层一次）的谱调制以约六分之一成本保住效果。SFMformer 在 5 个基准、3 个放大因子共 30 项 PSNR/SSIM 指标中有 28 项排名第一。

Conclusion: 在稀疏注意力 Transformer 中，选择质量与聚合质量应作为两个独立目标分别优化，前置空间增强与后置小波调制的配对在多数情况下产生超叠加收益，且低成本实现（每块一次谱调制、参数低于百万）即可取得领先性能，适合资源受限的边缘设备部署。

Abstract: Sparse attention mechanisms, which score all token pairs but propagate only the strongest, now underpin the most efficient Transformers for lightweight image super-resolution. This paper observes that sparsification changes what it means to improve such a network. A dense attention layer has one place where representation quality matters: the aggregation of attended features. A sparse layer has two, because the top-k operator first decides which tokens survive and only then decides what to do with them, and a token discarded at the selection stage cannot be recovered downstream. Selection quality and aggregation quality are therefore separable targets, addressed by modules placed before and after the attention respectively. We test this by pairing a dual-branch spatial enhancement on the input of a progressive focused attention with a wavelet-domain modulation on its output, forming SFMformer. Measuring each module alone and jointly over all fifteen benchmark-scale pairs, we find their gains are not additive: the joint gain exceeds the sum of the individual gains on nine pairs, and the sign of the discrepancy is predicted by how much the weaker module contributes on its own (r = -0.72), so the two compound when they relieve different constraints and overlap when they relieve the same one. Enabling spectral modulation once per block rather than once per layer retains the effect at roughly one-sixth of its cost, keeping the model below one million parameters at every scale. SFMformer ranks first on 28 of 30 PSNR/SSIM entries across five benchmarks and three upscaling factors. We report the cases where the pairing does not help, and deploy the model on a Raspberry Pi 5 to confirm the design is practical under tight resource budgets.

</details>


### [79] [LinCa: Accelerating Diffusion Models via Learnable Decomposed Feature Caching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17973)
*Jinshan Liu,Haoran Qin,Xiaobing Tu,Jiacheng Liu,Jiahui Hu,Zhengan Yan,Yukun Xie,Kerui Shen,Jinkui Ren,Yuqi Lin,Xiantao Zhang,Linfeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LinCa 是一个基于可学习可逆网络的特征缓存框架，通过将缓存特征分解为具有不同连续性的子成分并分配差异化的预测策略，在引入不到 0.2% 额外参数的情况下，实现扩散模型 5-7 倍加速且保持近乎无损的生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型在图像和视频生成中表现优异，但迭代采样的高计算成本限制了实际部署。现有免训练的特征缓存方法采用统一的预测策略，无法适应不同时间步上特征动态的异质性，导致在高加速比下生成质量严重下降。

Method: LinCa 提出可学习可逆网络的特征缓存框架：通过轻量可逆网络将缓存特征分解为具有不同连续性属性的子成分，并为每个成分匹配差异化的预测顺序，形成统一的"分解-预测-重建"（Decompose-Predict-Reconstruct）流水线。可逆性保证了无损重建回原始特征空间。同时为不同模型和时间步段训练独立的预测器，以适应异质性的特征动态。

Result: 在 FLUX、Qwen-Image 和 HunyuanVideo 上的实验表明，LinCa 仅引入不到 0.2% 的额外参数，显著优于现有方法，并在 5-7 倍加速下保持近乎无损的质量。

Conclusion: LinCa 通过可学习可逆网络实现特征分解与差异化预测，有效解决现有免训练缓存方法无法适应异质性特征动态的问题，在参数开销极小的情况下大幅超越现有方法，并实现高加速比下的近无损生成，为扩散模型的高效部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Diffusion models have achieved remarkable success in image and video generation, yet the high computational cost of iterative sampling remains a critical bottleneck for practical deployment. Feature caching has emerged as a promising acceleration paradigm by reusing or predicting intermediate features across timesteps. However, existing training-free methods apply uniform prediction strategies that cannot adapt to the heterogeneous feature dynamics, causing significant quality degradation under high acceleration ratios. We propose LinCa, a feature caching framework based on learnable invertible networks. LinCa decomposes cached features into sub-components with distinct continuity properties via a lightweight invertible network and applies differentiated prediction orders matched to each component. The strict invertibility guarantees lossless reconstruction back to the original feature space, forming a unified Decompose-Predict-Reconstruct pipeline. By training separate predictors for different models and timestep segments, LinCa adapts to heterogeneous feature dynamics. Experiments on FLUX, Qwen-Image, and HunyuanVideo demonstrate that LinCa, with less than 0.2% additional parameters, significantly outperforms existing methods and maintains near-lossless quality at 5-7x speedup. Code: https://github.com/QHR69/LinCa

</details>


### [80] [Dual Co-Train: Cross-Dataset Ultrasound Tongue Segmentation Under Extreme Data Scarcity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17983)
*Alisher Myrgyyassov,Zhen Song,Bruce Xiao Wang,Yu Sun,Min Ney Wong,Yihao Zhou,Yongping Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于轻量级UltraUNet的源数据无关（source-free）域自适应框架，用于超声舌部轮廓分割。该方法仅依赖5张标注源图像预训练，通过伪标签迭代精炼、轮廓质量控制过滤、分割引导的条件GAN生成目标风格合成样本，并采用一致性正则化训练学生模型，在8个超声舌部数据集的12个迁移对上验证，分割重叠率和轮廓精度均优于基线（含监督方法）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超声舌部轮廓分割在跨数据集域偏移下性能受限，具体障碍包括标注数据稀缺、探头差异和采集噪声，导致模型泛化能力下降。现有域自适应方法通常要求访问源数据，但在实际医疗场景中源数据常因隐私或存储限制不可用，因此需要一种源数据无关的域自适应方案。

Method: 该方法以轻量级UltraUNet为骨干，从仅有5张标注源图像训练的欠拟合约束源模型出发，对完全无标注的目标域进行自适应。流程包括：迭代精炼伪标签；用基于轮廓的质量控制模块过滤不可靠掩码；通过分割引导的条件GAN生成目标风格的合成图像-掩码对；将干净伪标签目标图像、含噪伪标签（一致性正则化）和合成样本混合训练学生模型，实现无需源数据的闭环自适应。

Result: 在8个超声舌部成像数据集的12个源-目标迁移对上评估，并进行源数据规模缩放实验和消融研究。结果显示，该框架在所有对比中均提升了分割重叠率和轮廓精度，且优于包括监督方法在内的基线。

Conclusion: 任务特定的伪标签精炼与目标风格合成增强能够显著提升超声舌部成像的源数据无关域自适应性能，验证了在源数据不可访问且标注稀缺条件下提升模型泛化能力的可行性。

Abstract: Ultrasound tongue contour segmentation remains challenging under cross-dataset domain shift, where limited annotations, probe variability, and acquisition noise often degrade model generalization. We present a source-free domain adaptation framework for robust ultrasound tongue segmentation built on a lightweight UltraUNet backbone. Starting from a checkpoint pretrained on only five labeled source images, simulating an underfitted constrained source model, the proposed method adapts to a fully-unlabeled target domain by iteratively refining pseudo-labels, filtering unreliable masks with a contour-based quality-control module, and generating target-style synthetic image-mask pairs through a segmentation-guided conditional GAN. The student model is then trained on a mixture of clean pseudo-labeled target images, noisy pseudo-labels with consistency regularization, and synthetic samples, enabling closed-loop adaptation without access to source data. We evaluate the method on 12 source-target transfer pairs across eight ultrasound tongue imaging datasets, and conduct source-size scaling experiments and ablation studies. Across all comparisons, the proposed framework improves segmentation overlap and contour accuracy over the baselines, including supervised ones. These results suggest that task-specific pseudo-label refinement and synthetic target-style augmentation can substantially improve source-free adaptation for ultrasound tongue imaging.

</details>


### [81] [GS-Voxel: Fitting-Free Structured Latents for Large-Scale 3DGS Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17988)
*Ming Qian,Zijian Wang,Minchao Sun,Jincheng Xiong,Hang Zhang,Mu Xu,Chi Wang,Baoquan Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GS-Voxel 提出一种免拟合的结构化潜在框架，将无序、空间不规则的预优化 3DGS 重建确定性地转换为稀疏活跃体素，并用因子化 VAE 编码到随占用体素数增长、不受固定基元数限制的稀疏 3D 潜空间，从而支持基于条件流模型的大规模航空 3D 高斯场景生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可扩展的潜在 3D 生成器多作用于结构化张量，而预优化的 3D Gaussian Splatting（3DGS）重建是无序的、空间不规则的，且基元数量差异巨大，难以直接作为结构化潜在表示用于大规模生成任务。

Method: GS-Voxel 确定性地将兼容的预优化 3DGS 重建转换为稀疏活跃体素，无需逐场景额外优化，并保留所选基元的亚体素位置与渲染属性；随后采用 GS 专用的因子化 VAE，将体素几何与局部高斯属性分别编码为稀疏 3D 潜变量，其尺寸随占用体素数量增长；在此潜空间上训练图像条件流模型，并通过重叠感知的分块推理实现超出单训练裁剪范围的大区域场景生成。

Result: 实验表明，GS-Voxel 能为预优化的航空 3DGS 重建提供结构化潜表示，且潜容量随占用体素数增长，并成功支撑基于卫星视图图像条件的大规模航空 3D 高斯场景生成。

Conclusion: GS-Voxel 弥合了无序 3DGS 重建与结构化潜生成之间的差距，为大规模航空 3D 高斯场景生成提供了可扩展、免拟合的潜在框架。

Abstract: Many scalable latent 3D generators operate on structured tensors, whereas pre-optimized 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) reconstructions are unordered, spatially irregular, and vary widely in primitive count. We present GS-Voxel, a fitting-free structured latent framework, and evaluate it for large-scale aerial 3D Gaussian scene generation. GS-Voxel deterministically converts a compatible pre-optimized 3DGS reconstruction into sparse active voxels without additional per-scene optimization, retaining the sub-voxel positions and rendering attributes of the selected primitives. A GS-specific factorized VAE then separately encodes voxel geometry and local Gaussian attributes into sparse 3D latents whose size grows with the number of occupied voxels rather than being limited by a fixed scene-wide primitive count. We train image-conditioned flow models in the GS-Voxel latent space to generate aerial 3DGS scenes. A key application enabled by GS-Voxel is large-area scene generation: overlap-aware tiled inference extends synthesis beyond a single training crop conditioned on satellite-view images. Our results show that GS-Voxel provides structured latents for pre-optimized aerial 3DGS reconstructions, with latent capacity that grows with the number of occupied voxels.

</details>


### [82] [AViTS: Adaptive Spatiotemporal Token Selection for Efficient Dynamic-Resolution Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17995)
*Haoran Qin,Zhengan Yan,Shikang Zheng,Xiaobing Tu,Jiacheng Liu,Yuqi Lin,Chang Zou,JinShan Liu,Peiliang Cai,Xiantao Zhang,Jinkui Ren,Linfeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AViTS 是一种面向动态分辨率 DiTs 的自适应时空 token 选择框架，通过融合潜变量-文本注意力的空间重要性与跨扩散步的 token 特征变化的时间重要性，实现选择性的分辨率提升，在 FLUX 上最高降低 6.34 倍、在 Qwen-Image-Edit 与 FLUX.1-Kontext-dev 上近 9 倍 FLOPs，并与蒸馏、量化、特征缓存正交，结合蒸馏模型可达 14.76 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DiT 迭代采样成本高昂；动态分辨率采样虽可降低早期成本，但在分辨率切换时对所有潜变量 token 统一上采样会带来冗余计算并可能损害细粒度一致性。现有部分上采样策略多依赖局部潜变量结构线索或单步统计，难以同时捕捉 token-文本的语义相关性以及跨扩散步的 token 级表示动态。

Method: 提出 AViTS 框架：用潜变量-文本注意力建模空间重要性，用跨扩散时间步的 token 级特征变化建模时间重要性，并将二者融合形成时空重要性感知的选择性上采样——优先对关键 token 进行分辨率细化，同时推迟次要 token 的细化。

Result: 在 FLUX 上 FLOPs 降低最高 6.34 倍，在 Qwen-Image-Edit 和 FLUX.1-Kontext-dev 上降低近 9 倍；该收益与蒸馏、量化、特征缓存等技术正交，结合蒸馏模型可达到 14.76 倍 FLOPs 降低。

Conclusion: AViTS 通过时空重要性感知的选择性上采样有效减少冗余高分辨率计算，显著改善了动态分辨率 DiT 的质量-效率权衡，并可与其他加速技术叠加进一步提升效率。

Abstract: Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) achieve high-quality generation but are costly due to iterative sampling. Dynamic-resolution sampling reduces early-stage cost by denoising at low resolution; however, uniformly upsampling all latent tokens at resolution transitions incurs redundant computation and may degrade fine-detail consistency. Existing partial upsampling strategies typically rely on local latent structure cues or single-step statistics, making it difficult to jointly capture token-text semantic relevance and token-wise representation dynamics across diffusion steps. We propose AViTS, an adaptive spatiotemporal token selection framework for dynamic-resolution DiTs. AViTS models spatial importance via latent-text attention and temporal importance via token-level feature variation across diffusion timesteps, and fuses them to enable spatiotemporal importance-aware selective upsampling: it prioritizes resolution refinement for critical tokens while deferring less important ones, thereby reducing redundant high-resolution computation and improving the quality-efficiency trade-off. AViTS achieves up to 6.34x on FLUX and nearly 9x FLOPs reduction on Qwen-Image-Edit and FLUX.1-Kontext-dev, orthogonal to distillation, quantization, and feature caching, and reaching 14.76x with distilled models. Code: https://github.com/QHR69/AViTS

</details>


### [83] [Memory Tree Guided Key Frame Querying for Efficient 3D Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18009)
*Hsiang-Wei Huang,Fu-Chen Chen,Li-Wu Tsao,Cheng-Han Lee,Che-Chun Su,Lu Xia,Ronghui Peng,Jenq-Neng Hwang,Min Sun,Cheng-Hao Kuo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 MemTree3D，一种基于记忆树引导的关键帧选择范式，利用紧凑可复用的 3D 场景表示，让 LLM 高效查询并检索问题相关关键帧，在 OpenEQA 上相比视觉搜索方法显著提升准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身场景中 VLM 推理受限于计算与内存资源，现有视觉搜索关键帧检索方法需对每个查询在数千视频帧中进行视觉搜索，效率低下。

Method: 提出 MemTree3D，一种紧凑可复用的 3D 场景表示，利用相机 6-DoF 位姿支持实时在线构建；MemTree3D 捕获多层级 3D 场景信息，使大语言模型通过基于评分的帧选择机制高效查询并检索问题相关的关键帧，无需重新处理整个视频流。

Result: 在 OpenEQA 基准上，方法使 GPT-4o 的 LLM-Match 提升 17.4%，LLaVA-OneVision-7B 提升 5.8%，优于现有视觉搜索方法。

Conclusion: 基于记忆树引导的关键帧选择范式通过紧凑可复用的 3D 场景表示，实现了具身 3D 问答的高效与准确，代码已开源。

Abstract: Answering questions accurately and efficiently in embodied scenarios presents significant challenges due to limited computational and memory resources for Vision Language Model (VLM) inference. Existing methods adopt visual search key frame retrieval method to select critical question-related key frames for VLM input. However, visual search methods are inefficient because they require visual search among thousands of video frames for each individual user query. In this work, we propose a memory tree guided key frame selection paradigm for efficient 3D question answering in embodied scenarios. Our method leverages a compact and reusable 3D scene representation, termed MemTree3D, which supports real-time online construction leveraging camera 6-DoF poses. MemTree3D captures multi-level 3D scene information, enabling a Large Language Model to efficiently query and retrieve question-relevant key frames through our scoring-based frame selection without reprocessing the entire video stream. On OpenEQA, our method improves the LLM-Match of GPT-4o by 17.4%, LLaVA-OneVision-7B by 5.8%, outperforms existing visual search methods. Our code is available at https://github.com/hsiangwei0903/MemTree3D

</details>


### [84] [Automated ACL Footprint Identification Using 3D Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18012)
*Ruida Cheng,Ali Uneri,Gabriel Gibson,Frances T. Sheehan,Barry Boden*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究探索了两种3D深度学习方法用于直接从3D磁共振图像中识别前交叉韧带（ACL）股骨足迹中心：基于3D图卷积神经网络的几何模型与基于3D MR图像的标志点增强识别模型。结果显示图像法（平均误差2.1mm）优于模型法（2.8mm），证明3D深度学习可为ACL重建的足迹定位提供可行的临床方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ACL重建失败最常见原因之一是股骨隧道定位不当（ACL足迹中心与隧道方向），可能导致半月板病变和骨关节炎。精准识别ACL股骨足迹对精确置钉、恢复原生膝关节力学、术后关节健康及预防移植物失败至关重要。然而现有AI研究主要聚焦于术前/术后MR图像的ACL分割与断裂分类，利用深度学习识别ACL足迹中心尚未被深入研究，故本研究的动机在于填补这一空白。

Method: 开发了两种综合3D深度学习架构：（1）基于3D图卷积神经网络（GCN）的几何模型，应用于3D股骨网格；（2）基于3D MR图像的标志点增强识别模型。使用公开数据库共4883个右膝和3087个左膝图像集，80%用于模型生成/训练，20%保留用于模型测试。

Result: 两种模型均取得优异性能，但基于图像的方法优于基于模型的方法：平均误差2.1mm对比2.8mm。

Conclusion: 3D深度学习为ACL足迹定位提供了一种可行的临床方法，并有望提升ACL重建中足迹定位的准确性。

Abstract: One of the most common reasons for anterior cruciate ligament (ACL) reconstruction failure is femoral tunnel malpositioning (ACL footprint center and tunnel orientation). Such failures may lead to the development of meniscal pathology and osteoarthritis. Accurate ACL femoral footprint identification is therefore essential for precise tunnel placement, restoration of the native knee joint mechanics, post-surgical knee joint health and prevention of graft failure. Recent advances in artificial intelligence (AI) bring new opportunities to improve image-guided orthopedic surgery. However, at present, existing AI research focuses primarily on ACL segmentation and rupture classification based on pre- and post-operative magnetic resonance (MR) images. Identification of the ACL footprint center using deep learning methods has not been thoroughly researched. Thus, the purpose of this study is to explore 3D deep learning models for ACL femoral footprint identification directly from 3D MR images. Two comprehensive 3D deep learning architectures were developed: a 3D graph convolutional neural network-based geometric model applied to 3D femoral meshes; and a 3D landmark-enhanced identification model based on 3D MR images. A total of 4883 right and 3087 left knee image sets were used from a publicly available database. Eighty percent (80%) were applied to model generation, and twenty percent (20%) were preserved for model testing. Both models achieved excellent performance; however, the image-based method outperformed the model-based method (average error of 2.1mm vs 2.8 mm). Thus, 3D deep learning provides a feasible clinical approach for ACL footprint localization and has the potential to improve ACL reconstruction footprint accuracy.

</details>


### [85] [Initialization-Free Bundle Adjustment Revisited: A Controlled Experimental Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18028)
*Simon Weber,Mateo de Mayo,Je Hyeong Hong,Carl Olsson,Daniel Cremers,Ronald Clark*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文重新审视免初始化光束法平差（InitFree BA），发现现有方法存在被忽视的"优化-重建鸿沟"：即便OSE目标值相近，投影解经度量升级后仍可能产生差异巨大的欧氏重建。作者通过统一评测框架（C++实现 + Blender数据集生成器）揭示影响重建成功的关键因素，并为该领域奠定更扎实的实验基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有InitFree BA方法（基于OSE与VarPro）虽在优化行为上表现良好，但评测仅关注优化是否成功，未回答"低OSE目标值是否意味着得到有效的度量三维重建"这一核心问题。作者旨在澄清优化成功与重建有效性之间的真实关系。

Method: 构建统一评测框架：①用C++实现现有OSE公式；②开发基于Blender的数据集生成器，提供精确地面真值、可控相机配置与观测密度。在此框架下对InitFree BA进行实验性重访，系统比较不同投影解在度量升级后的欧氏重建质量。

Result: 实验揭示"优化-重建鸿沟"：具有相近低OSE目标值的投影解，经度量升级后可能得到显著不同的欧氏重建。关键影响因素包括：初始化先验、地标观测密度、度量升级稳定性。结果表明InitFree BA的主要挑战并非最小化OSE目标，而是获得可可靠升为度量重建的投影解。

Conclusion: InitFree BA的核心难点在于获取支持可靠度量升级的投影重建，而非单纯优化OSE目标。作者提出的基准、实现与分析为该领域提供了更强的实验基础，期望推动这一在计算机视觉界探索不足的问题的后续研究。

Abstract: Initialization-free bundle adjustment (InitFree BA) aims to recover camera poses and scene structure directly from image observations, avoiding the geometric initialization stages of conventional structure-from-motion pipelines. Recent methods based on Object-Space Error (OSE) formulations and Variable Projection (VarPro) show encouraging optimization behavior from random camera configurations. However, existing evaluations primarily measure optimization success, leaving unclear whether a low OSE objective yields a valid metric 3D reconstruction. We revisit InitFree BA experimentally through a unified evaluation framework combining a C++ implementation of existing OSE formulations with a Blender-based dataset generator providing exact ground truth and controlled camera configurations and observation densities. Our experiments reveal a previously overlooked optimization--reconstruction gap: projective solutions with similarly low OSE values can lead to substantially different Euclidean reconstructions after metric upgrade. We identify initialization priors, landmark observation density, and metric-upgrade stability as key factors governing reconstruction success. Overall, our results suggest that the main challenge of InitFree BA is not merely minimizing OSE objectives, but obtaining projective reconstructions that admit reliable metric upgrade. We believe that the proposed benchmark, implementation, and analysis establish stronger experimental foundations for future research on initialization-free bundle adjustment, a problem largely unexplored within the computer vision community. Project page is available at https://github.com/simonwebertum/InitFreeBA.git.

</details>


### [86] [Deep Academic Survey: Stateful Agentic Closed-Loop Paradigm for Academic Survey Automation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18034)
*Zhikai Xu,Zhucun Xue,Teng Hu,Yabiao Wang,Yong Liu,Jiangning Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DAS 是一个面向出版级学术综述生成的有状态智能体框架，通过分离可复用的论文分析与特定主题的稿件构建，并维护显式的文献、组织、写作与定稿状态，在四个评估维度和专家评审上均优于现有系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学术综述对快速扩张的文献整理至关重要，但其构建需要深入的论文分析、连贯的知识组织、细粒度的引用支撑和可靠的稿件组装。现有 Deep Research 及自动化综述系统只覆盖部分环节，缺乏通过共享且可修订的状态来协调论文理解、文献组织、基于证据的撰写和稿件验证。

Method: 提出 DAS，将有状态的智能体框架分为两阶段：先复用论文分析，再做主题相关稿件构建。其基于 DAS-2M（约 200 万篇论文的动态元数据湖），通过候选锚定的分类规划（candidate-grounded taxonomy planning）、反向论文到章节路由（reverse paper-to-section routing）、层级主张与引用规划（hierarchical claim and citation planning）来维护显式的文献、组织、写作与定稿状态；语义评审（semantic review）只重新激活受影响的写作状态进行修复与重评估，形成带有确定性验证的受限闭环。

Result: 在 30 个主题上，DAS 在学术引用质量、分类综合、层级论述与稿件组装可靠性四个维度均取得最高平均分，总分 4.34，优于最强竞争系统的 4.03；在匹配的 21 个 CS 主题子集上保持相同排序。双盲专家评审在 30 个主题中的 27 个偏好 DAS 而非 Naive RAG，在 21 个共享 CS 主题中的 19 个偏好 DAS 而非 AutoSurvey。

Conclusion: DAS 通过有状态智能体架构和共享可修订状态，将论文理解、文献组织、证据支撑撰写与稿件验证协同起来，显著提升了自动化综述的引用质量、分类综合、层级论述与装配可靠性，并在基准测试与真人评审中全面领先。

Abstract: Academic surveys play a central role in organizing rapidly expanding scholarly literature, yet their construction requires extensive paper analysis, coherent knowledge organization, fine-grained citation support, and reliable manuscript assembly. Existing Deep Research and automated survey generation systems address parts of this process, but typically do not coordinate paper understanding, literature organization, evidence-grounded drafting, and manuscript validation through a shared, revisable state. We introduce DAS, a stateful agentic framework for generating publication-oriented academic surveys. Its key idea is to separate reusable paper analysis from topic-specific manuscript construction. DAS builds on DAS-2M, a dynamically updated metadata lake containing survey-oriented representations of approximately two million papers. Its agents maintain explicit literature, organization, writing, and finalization states through candidate-grounded taxonomy planning, reverse paper-to-section routing, and hierarchical claim and citation planning. Semantic review reactivates only the affected writing states for repair and reevaluation, forming a scoped closed loop with deterministic validation. We further introduce DAS-Bench, a 30-topic benchmark, together with DAS-Eval, which assesses scholarly citation quality, taxonomic synthesis, hierarchical discourse, and manuscript assembly reliability through 16 criteria. Among systems evaluated on all 30 topics, DAS achieves the highest average in all four dimensions, with an overall score of 4.34 compared with 4.03 for the strongest competitor, and the same ordering is preserved on the matched 21-topic CS subset. Blinded expert evaluation further prefers DAS to Naive RAG on 27 of 30 topics and to AutoSurvey on 19 of 21 shared CS topics. The project page is available at https://zhikaixu24.github.io/projects/DAS/.

</details>


### [87] [Plug-and-Play Traffic Element Awareness for End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18035)
*Zongzheng Zhang,Jijun Wang,Saining Zhang,Shuo Wang,Yiru Wang,Hai Yang,Yang Chen,Yuwen Heng,Hao Sun,Anqing Jiang,Hao Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文首次系统研究交通元素（如交通灯、路标）感知对端到端自动驾驶的影响，提出一种即插即用的通用集成方案，并在多个基准上验证其能一致提升驾驶性能、在 NAVSIM-v2 上刷新最高纪录。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交通元素对人类驾驶决策至关重要，但现有端到端驾驶研究主要关注动态道路参与者（车辆、行人），而交通元素的作用被忽视。社区缺乏系统量化其影响的研究，原因在于公共数据集缺少结构化的交通元素标注，且现代驾驶系统架构与训练范式差异巨大。

Method: 构建统一研究基础设施，为多个公共驾驶数据集补充全面的交通元素标注；采用最小化且通用的集成设计，以即插即用方式将交通元素信号融入现有流程，几乎无需架构修改；在感知-预测-规划管线、视觉-语言-动作模型（VLA）、回归规划器、扩散策略、轨迹评分框架等现代范式上评估，覆盖 nuScenes、NAVSIM-v1、NAVSIM-v2 和 Bench2Drive。

Result: 在所有范式和数据集上，该简单集成一致提升驾驶性能，表明交通元素感知为端到端驾驶系统提供了稳健且可泛化的信号；在具有挑战性的 NAVSIM-v2 基准上，方法显著改进最先进架构和数据管线，建立新的最优水平。

Conclusion: 交通元素感知是提升端到端自动驾驶性能的通用有效信号，可低成本集成到多种现代驾驶架构中并带来稳定增益，未来可进一步探索其与更多驾驶要素的融合。

Abstract: Traffic elements such as traffic lights and road signs play a fundamental role in human driving decisions and should naturally influence end-to-end driving performance. However, existing end-to-end driving research predominantly focuses on dynamic road participants (e.g., vehicles and pedestrians), while the role of traffic elements remains largely unexplored. The community still lacks a systematic study quantifying their impact, largely because public datasets rarely provide structured traffic-element annotations and modern driving systems vary widely in architecture and training paradigm. In this work, we present the first systematic investigation of traffic element awareness for end-to-end autonomous driving. We construct a unified research infrastructure by augmenting multiple public driving datasets with comprehensive traffic-element annotations.
