### [120] [Preference Is Not Intervention: The Structure and Stability Boundaries of Reader-Specific Evidence Utility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17781)
*Shi Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究RAG系统中不同下游模型（reader）对证据效用的异质性：九个reader在33%的联合受影响单元上效应符号不一致，且reader×query交互解释了29.8%的效用方差（远超8.4%的置换零假设）。进一步区分证据活跃性、序数偏好与条件符号方向三个对象后，发现序数几何在四个独立设置间稳定，但符号几何受任务类型约束（开放问答弱、二元事实核查强）。关键结论：稳定的序数相似性并不能预测跨reader的干预迁移，即"偏好≠干预"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ML系统日益依赖下游模型身份做决策，但这仅在模型间差异构成可复用结构（而非输入局部交互）时才有价值。作者通过RAG中可控干预下的证据效用测量，检验并分解这种异质性的可复用性，追问"哪些异质性成分是跨query稳定的reader属性"。

Method: 以检索增强生成为实验场景，固定query、evidence、task、scoring与intervention，测量九个reader的效应符号一致性；分解异质性方差（reader×query交互 vs 置换零假设）；分离三个可测对象——证据活跃性（activity）、序数偏好（ordinal preference）、条件符号方向（conditional signed direction）；在四个独立设置（leave-one-out干预、PRISM偏好、RAMDocs、RAGuard）中用split-half ρ评估几何稳定性；用oracle-distance ρ与regret reliability检验跨reader干预迁移，并排除稀疏性、解码噪声与度量伪影的干扰。

Result: 九个reader在33%的联合受影响单元上效应符号不一致；reader×query交互解释29.8%效用方差（置换零假设仅8.4%）；自选证据使F1提升+0.031（t=3.39）。序数reader几何在四设置间稳定（split-half ρ=0.60–0.83）；符号几何受任务约束：开放问答弱（0.14、0.35，尤以误导性/无关证据为甚），二元事实核查强（0.75）且无显著序数差距，但仍低于其稀疏匹配上限。跨reader干预迁移失败：oracle-distance ρ=-0.27，regret reliability -0.28。

Conclusion: Reader特定的效用确实存在，但偏好并非干预：稳定的排序相似性不能授权帮助/伤害决策的迁移。模型间差异中可复用的是序数偏好几何，而符号方向及其干预后果是任务限定且不可迁移的，这对依赖下游模型身份做决策的实践构成重要警示。

Abstract: ML systems increasingly condition decisions on downstream model identity, but this is useful only if model-specific differences form reusable structure rather than input-local interactions. We test this in retrieval-augmented generation (RAG), where evidence utility can be measured under controlled interventions. Holding query, evidence, task, scoring, and intervention fixed, nine readers disagree on effect sign in 33\% of jointly affected cells; reader$\times$query interaction explains 29.8\% of utility variance versus an 8.4\% permutation null; and self-selected evidence improves F1 by $+0.031$ ($t=3.39$). We then ask the sharper question: \emph{which components of this heterogeneity are stable reader properties across queries?} Separating three measurable objects---evidence \emph{activity}, \emph{ordinal preference}, and \emph{conditional signed direction}---we find ordinal reader geometry stable across four independent settings (split-half $ρ=0.60$--$0.83$): leave-one-out interventions, PRISM preferences, RAMDocs, and RAGuard. Signed geometry is task-bounded: weak in open-ended QA (0.14, 0.35), especially for misleading and irrelevant evidence, but strong in binary fact-checking (0.75) with no significant ordinal gap, though still below its sparsity-matched ceiling. Sparsity, decoding noise, and metric artifacts do not explain the main ordinal--signed gap. Finally, stable ordinal similarity fails to predict cross-reader intervention transfer (oracle-distance $ρ=-0.27$; regret reliability $-0.28$). Reader-specific utility exists, but preference is not intervention: stable ranking similarity does not license transfer of help/harm decisions.

</details>


### [121] [TraceSQL: Traceable Answerability Estimation for Reference-Free Text-to-SQL Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17795)
*Neelesh Kumar Shukla,Debasmita Panda,Srutanik Bhaduri,Aditya Banerjee,Viji Krishnamurthy*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 现有 Text-to-SQL 验证方法（LLM 判别器、ORM）难以追溯决策依据，本文提出基于 67 个显式诊断特征的轻量可追溯验证模型 TraceSQL，在 BIRD 上以 66.47% F1 超越 GradeSQL-7B 基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Text-to-SQL 系统在真实部署中推理时缺乏 ground-truth SQL 或参考执行结果，因此需要一个仅凭用户问题、数据库上下文和生成 SQL 即可判断查询是否可能正确的验证机制。现有方法中，LLM 判别器或专用智能体的决策难以追溯，而 Outcome Reward Models（ORM）虽能从执行标注中学习并打分，但同样缺乏对每个验证判断背后信号的可见性。

Method: 提出 TraceSQL：一个基于显式诊断特征的轻量、可追溯验证模型。模型组合了 67 个特征，涵盖问题歧义、问题需求、问题-模式-SQL 一致性、SQL 结构以及意图对齐等维度。这些信号在整个流程中保持可用，既可用来分析影响每个预测的因素，也可将决策追溯回具体的诊断证据。

Result: 在 BIRD 开发数据库上，TraceSQL 取得 66.47% F1 和 64.48% ROC-AUC，优于同等生成 SQL 评估下的 GradeSQL-7B ORM 基线（61.87% F1 和 58.26% ROC-AUC）。特征归因分析显示，模型同时依赖语义 grounding 信号和确定性的 SQL 结构信号。

Conclusion: SQL 验证可以由轻量级学习模型完成，同时保留特征级证据用于检查和诊断其预测。这一结果表明，无需大型 ORM 也可实现可追溯、可解释的 SQL 正确性验证，且性能更优。

Abstract: Text-to-SQL systems are commonly evaluated using ground-truth SQL queries or reference execution results, but such supervision is unavailable at inference time in real-world deployments. This creates a critical verification problem: given only a user question, database context, and generated SQL, can a system estimate whether the generated query is likely to correctly answer the question? Recent approaches use LLMs as judge or specialized agents to inspect generated SQL, but their decisions can be difficult to trace. Outcome Reward Models (ORMs) address this by learning from execution-labeled candidate SQLs and assigning correctness scores to unseen queries, yet they still provide limited visibility into the signals behind each verification. To address this limitation, we propose TraceSQL, a lightweight and traceable verification model built on explicit diagnostic features. TraceSQL combines 67 features capturing question ambiguity, question requirements, question-schema-SQL consistency, SQL structure, and intent alignment. These signals remain available for examining which factors influence each prediction and for tracing decisions back to diagnostic evidence. On BIRD development databases, TraceSQL achieves 66.47% F1 and 64.48% ROC-AUC, compared with 61.87% F1 and 58.26% ROC-AUC for the GradeSQL-7B ORM baseline on the same generated-SQL evaluation. Feature attribution further shows that the model relies on both semantic grounding and deterministic SQL-structure signals. These results show that SQL verification can be performed with a lightweight learned model while retaining feature-level evidence for inspecting and diagnosing its predictions.

</details>


### [122] [Whether LLMs Can Navigate Beliefs and Facts Depends on How You Phrase It](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17809)
*Quang Minh Nguyen,Luis Frentzen Salim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了LLM在处理用户信念（belief）与事实交织时的系统性缺陷，发现模型常将"信念确认"任务误判为"事实核查"任务，且该缺陷随表达信念的动词（epistemic expression）不同而方向和大小各异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类在日常交流中自然表达信念（如"I think…"），这些信念与事实、知识紧密交织。随着LLM被广泛部署于面向用户的场景，模型需要同时处理信念与事实。先前工作显示即使能力较强的LLM在确认基于错误信息的用户信念时也存在系统性弱点，本文进一步探究这一现象的来源、规模与机制。

Method: 将评估扩展至10个LLM、覆盖18种认知动词（epistemic expressions），测量事实信息与虚假信息之间的准确率差距；通过对比"显式事实核查"与"非事实核查"的思维链（chain of thought）分析任务混淆；使用单条指令干预检验可逆性；并利用注意力分析与解码时注意力抑制（attention suppression）探索机制层面的因果关系。

Result: 准确率差距随动词而变化：从"I vaguely remember"的+50%到"I seriously doubt"的-14%。该现象源于任务混淆：模型默认对底层陈述进行事实核查，从而覆盖了用户明确表达的信念；显式进行事实核查的思维链在错误信息上的准确率更低；单条指令即可在各类动词族上逆转该缺陷。机制上，模型对未能确认的错误信念给予更多注意力，但在解码时抑制该注意力只能部分恢复准确率，且仅在部分模型中有效。

Conclusion: 本文澄清了先前研究结果，并揭示了事实核查——这一通常被视为理想的行为——会干扰LLM的信念追踪（belief tracking）能力。同时指出需继续研究更有效的干预方法。

Abstract: Humans naturally form and express beliefs in daily communication, e.g., "I think the answer is 3" or "I suppose that's right." Such beliefs inevitably intertwine with fact and knowledge, making the ability to handle them in tandem desirable for large language models (LLMs), as they are increasingly deployed in user-facing settings. Prior work showed that even capable LLMs exhibit a systemic weakness in acknowledging user beliefs grounded in incorrect information. We extend this evaluation to 10 LLMs across 18 epistemic expressions and find that the size and direction of the weakness depend on the verb used to express the belief, with the accuracy gap between factual and false information ranging from +50% on "I vaguely remember" to -14% on "I seriously doubt". We further show that the phenomenon stems from task confusion: models default to fact-checking the underlying claim, overriding the user's stated belief; chains of thought that explicitly fact-check show lower accuracy on false information than those that do not; and a single instruction can reverse the failure across verb families. Mechanistically, models attend more to false beliefs they fail to confirm, but suppressing this attention at decoding time recovers accuracy only partially and only in some models, calling for future work on intervention methods. Our findings clarify prior results and show how fact-checking, a generally desirable behavior, can interfere with belief tracking in LLMs. Our code is available at https://github.com/ngqm/belief-fact-phrasing.

</details>


### [123] [Interpretable Humans, Alien LLMs: Expert Analysis of Latent Structures in Assessment Responses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17810)
*Alona Strugatski,Licol Zeinfeld,Jason Cooper,Shelley Rap,Gil Schwarts,Giora Alexandron*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文质疑“AI 与人类共享相似认知结构”这一隐含假设，通过探索性因子分析（EFA）与学科专家盲评相结合，发现 LLM 的潜在因子大多难以被赋予人类可解释的教学意义，表明 LLM 的运作机制在统计上常常与人类推理不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 评估依赖人类设计的测评题，隐含假设 AI 与人类采用相似的底层认知结构。作者挑战这一假设，旨在探究支配 LLM 表现的潜在因子是否与支配人类学习者的认知结构具有相同的实质性与人类可解释意义。

Method: 收集人类与六个 LLM 在定量推理和化学测评上的作答数据，分别对两组进行探索性因子分析（EFA）；随后由学科专家（SME）在不告知因子来源的情况下盲评所得到的因子图，并尝试为浮现出的因子赋予教学意义。

Result: 专家能成功解释大多数人类衍生的因子；反之，在定量推理中专家无法为任何 LLM 衍生因子赋予意义，在化学中也只能解释约一半的 LLM 因子。

Conclusion: 通过将数据驱动的 EFA 与盲法专家解读相结合，该框架表明 LLM 常常依赖统计上不透明、且与人类推理不同的机制运行，这对仅基于人类认知假设的 LLM 评估方式提出了根本性质疑。

Abstract: The evaluation of large language models (LLMs) relies heavily on human-designed assessments, implicitly assuming that AI and humans employ similar underlying cognitive constructs. Challenging this assumption, we investigate whether the latent factors governing LLM performance carry the same substantive, human-interpretable meaning as the cognitive constructs governing human learners. Using responses from humans and six LLMs across quantitative reasoning and chemistry assessments, we conducted Exploratory Factor Analysis (EFA) separately for both groups. Subject-Matter Experts (SMEs) then blindly evaluated the resulting factor graphs to ascribe pedagogical meaning to the emerged constructs. SMEs successfully interpreted most of the human-derived factors. Conversely, they could not ascribe meaning to any LLM-derived factors in quantitative reasoning and interpreted only half of the LLM factors in chemistry. By combining data-driven EFA with blind expert interpretation, this framework shows that LLMs frequently operate on statistically opaque mechanisms distinct from human reasoning.

</details>


### [124] [From Global Benchmarks to Local Evaluations: Benchmarking LLMs for the German Public Sector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17827)
*Camilla Dalerci,Thilo Michael,Robin Schaefer,Daniel Weinland*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出面向德国公共部门的LLM评估框架MÖVE，除任务性能外还考察能耗、供应商透明度及对德国政党立场的知识三个治理维度，发现各模型无全面占优，模型选择不能仅依赖性能排名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公共机构面临为特定场景选择合适LLM的持续难题，而现有基准主要反映英语及美国中心情境，且大多只评估任务性能，难以满足德国公共部门的治理需求。

Method: 提出MÖVE整体评估框架，针对德国公共部门，在任务性能之外引入三个通常被忽视的治理维度：能耗估算、供应商透明度、以及模型对德国政党立场的知识掌握程度，并报告首批评估结果。

Result: 各维度间存在显著权衡，无一模型在所有维度上全面领先：估算能耗差异超过60倍且无法仅用模型规模解释；信息披露在不同供应商间存在系统性差异；欧洲模型在德国政党立场知识上并不更强。

Conclusion: 公共机构的模型选择不能仅依赖性能排名，评估应同时反映部署场景的治理要求。

Abstract: Public institutions face a persistent challenge in selecting LLMs suited to their specific context. Existing benchmarks, however, are of limited use as they primarily reflect English-language and US-centric settings, and often only evaluate task performance. In this paper, we present first results of MÖVE, a holistic evaluation framework for the German public sector, examining three rarely considered governance dimensions: energy consumption, provider transparency, and knowledge of German-party positions. Our results reveal significant trade-offs, with no single model excelling across all dimensions: estimated energy consumption varies more than 60-fold and is not explained by model size alone, information disclosure varies systematically across providers, and European models do not exhibit stronger knowledge of German party positions. Model selection for public institutions thus cannot rely on performance rankings alone. Instead, evaluations should also reflect the governance requirements of the deployment context.

</details>


### [125] [Encoded but Not Actionable: Auditing the Decode-Generate-Steer Gap in Frozen LLMs for Geometric Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17843)
*Man Liang,Xinzhao Cheng,Faizan Wajid*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过参数化CAD几何约束这一受控测试平台，探究LLM内部编码的几何信息与其行为之间的关系。作者对6个冻结的解码器模型进行探针分析，发现预训练显著提升局部几何关系的可解码性，但草图级自由度状态即使在随机初始化下也已高度可解码。更重要的是，可解码的信息并不总是可表达或可操控：生成常常无法表达编码信息，激活层面的影响消失而可解码性持续存在，均值差操控也不可靠。研究表明解码性、生成、激活影响与可操控性会相互背离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在结构化推理任务上表现强劲，但其内部编码了什么、以及这些编码是否真正驱动模型行为仍不清楚。作者希望通过几何推理任务，将局部成对关系与草图级约束状态分离开来，从而系统地区分"编码失败"与"表达/控制失败"。

Method: 以参数化CAD约束为受控测试平台，对6个冻结的解码器-only LLM的隐藏状态进行探针分析，考察四个属性：线性可解码性、强制选择生成、激活层级影响、行为可操控性。采用乱序控制（shuffled-order controls）来排除位置线索的影响，并进行激活-恢复实验和均值差操控（mean-difference steering）实验。

Result: 预训练显著提升局部几何关系的解码能力，且在排除位置线索后优势仍存；而草图级DOF状态在随机初始化表示下就已高度可解码，预训练仅带来小幅提升。可解码信息并不总是可执行：生成常无法表达该信息；在两个接受干预测试的主干模型上，修补实体位置处的激活-恢复效应随深度消失，而可解码性持续存在；均值差操控亦无法可靠控制输出。

Conclusion: 在受测场景中，可解码性、生成表现、激活层级影响与可操控性可以相互背离。该审计方法提供了一种受控途径，用以区分模型是未能编码几何结构，还是未能表达或控制已编码的信息。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated strong performance on structured reasoning tasks, but what they encode and whether it informs model behavior remain unclear. We investigate this question through geometric reasoning, using parametric CAD constraints as a controlled testbed for separating local pairwise relations from sketch-level constraint status. By probing the hidden states of six frozen decoder-only LLMs, we examine four properties: linear decodability, forced-choice generation, activation-level influence, and behavioral steerability. Pretraining substantially improves the decoding of local geometric relations, and this advantage persists after accounting for positional cues with shuffled-order controls. In contrast, sketch-level DOF status is already highly decodable from randomly initialized representations and improves only modestly with pretraining, indicating that much of its probe performance is available without learned weights. Further analyses show that decodable information is not always actionable. Generation often fails to express this information, and on the two intervention-tested backbones, activation-restoration effects at the patched entity position vanish while decodability persists across depth. Mean-difference steering also does not reliably control outputs. These results show that decodability, generation, activation-level influence, and steerability can diverge in the tested setting. The audit provides a controlled way to distinguish failures to encode geometric structure from failures to express or control encoded information.

</details>


### [126] [BayesPrompt: human readable prompts that make sense](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17866)
*Franky Kevin Nando Tezoh,Ali Hussaini Umar,Alessandro Laio,Guido Sanguinetti,Riccardo Rende*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文指出直接最小化困惑度的提示优化任务是不适定的，会产生难以理解的"伪提示"；作者将其重构为提示上的贝叶斯后验推断，提出一种高效采样算法，能够同时获得高效率和可读性强的提示，并在真实数据集上多项指标显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有以最小化目标答案困惑度为目标的提示优化方法，普遍会收敛到"伪提示"——即缺乏人类可理解性的乱码式 token 串。作者认为这一现象源于提示优化任务本身的不适定性（ill-posedness），因此需要从根本上重新定义优化目标，以在提示效率与人类可读性之间取得平衡。

Method: 作者放弃点估计式的困惑度最小化，将提示重构任务重新表述为在提示空间上的贝叶斯后验推断问题，并提出一种高效的采样算法，从后验分布中抽取既具有低困惑度（高效率）又满足人类可读性约束的提示。

Result: 在真实数据集上，与当前最先进的替代方法相比，该方法在一系列评估指标上均取得了显著改进，验证了贝叶斯后验采样框架在兼顾提示有效性与可读性方面的优越性。

Conclusion: 提示优化面临的不适定性是产生伪提示的根源；通过贝叶斯后验推断并配合高效采样，能够有效生成高可读且高效的提示，为该领域提供了一个更有前景的新方向。

Abstract: Reconstructing prompts that can elicit a desired answer or behaviour in an LLM is an open and important research topic. Optimisation methods which aim at minimising the perplexity of a given answer, however, consistently yield so-called pseudoprompts, unintelligible strings of tokens which can lack human interpretability. We argue that this is a consequence of the ill-posedness of the prompt optimisation task. By reframing the task as a Bayesian posterior inference over prompts, we propose an efficient algorithm to sample prompts which are both efficient (in terms of perplexity) and human readable. We compare our approach with state of the art alternatives showing on a real data set a marked improvement over a range of metrics.

</details>


### [127] [BEAR-Bench: A Bilingual Enterprise and Academic Reasoning Benchmark for Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17895)
*Liubov Chubarova,Alexandra Kuleshova,Daniil Volkov,Kirill Sultanov,Alexey Zaytsev*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了BEAR-Bench，一个英俄双语、基于文本密集型商业与科学文档的双语企业与学术推理基准，包含1000道人工标注问题，并评估16个专有与开源多模态大模型，发现即便最强的系统也仍有明显改进空间，同时用模型输出对比了现有幻觉检测方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态大模型在专业、文本密集文档上的推理能力评估不足：已有基准偏重信息抽取、依赖外部领域知识，或仅将专业文档作为一种次要场景；同时它们大多以英语或中文为中心，俄语等语言严重缺乏代表性。

Method: 构建自包含的英俄双语基准BEAR-Bench，基于文本丰富的商业和科学文档，人工标注1000道问题；在基准上评测16个专有与开源多模态大模型（如Gemini 3.1 Pro、Qwen3.5-397B）；并利用模型输出对现有幻觉检测方法进行比较分析。

Result: 在BEAR-Bench上，即使是最强的多模态大模型也表现出明显的性能空间（清晰的头部分）；同时，对幻觉检测方法的对比评估揭示了模型失败被可靠识别的程度。

Conclusion: BEAR-Bench填补了专业文档推理、复杂问题及俄语覆盖方面的空白，揭示了当前多模态大模型在文本密集专业文档推理上仍有显著提升空间，并为幻觉检测的可靠性提供了评估依据。

Abstract: While Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have made significant strides in visual comprehension, their ability to reason about text-dense, professional documents remains incompletely evaluated. Existing benchmarks emphasize information extraction, require external domain knowledge, or cover professional documents only as one of many settings. They are also largely English- or Chinese-centric, leaving other languages and Russian, in particular, substantially underrepresented. To address these limitations, we introduce BEAR-Bench (Bilingual Enterprise and Academic Reasoning), a self-contained, complex English-and-Russian benchmark comprising 1000 human-annotated questions based on text-rich business and scientific documents. We evaluate 16 proprietary and open-weight MLLMs, including Gemini 3.1 Pro and Qwen3.5-397B, on BEAR-Bench and observe clear headroom even for the strongest systems. Finally, we use the resulting model outputs to compare existing hallucination detection methods, evaluating not only how often models fail on BEAR-Bench but also how reliably those failures can be identified.

</details>


### [128] [CABLE: Extending the Reach of Memory Retrieval via Complementary Antecedent-Based Linking and Expansion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17911)
*Zheling Tan,Jin Gao,Dequan Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CABLE 是一种记忆增强方法,通过构建稀疏且与宿主检索器互补的"前因链接"图,扩展语义检索的直接覆盖范围,从而在长期会话记忆中恢复语义上相距较远但却相关的潜在证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期会话记忆面临"证据可达性"问题:现有检索主要依赖语义相似度,擅长主题性召回,但会遗漏那些与后续事件语义距离较远、却能解释后者形成原因的更早经历、计划或动机。现有记忆图虽然提供跨记忆结构,但其链接主要由语义重叠驱动,容易与宿主检索器已召回的内容重复。

Method: 提出 CABLE(Complementary Antecedent-Based Linking and Expansion)插件式增强方案:对每条新记忆生成以"前因"为导向的查询并检索先前记忆;剔除位于直接语义邻域内的候选;对剩余候选进行验证后,将接受的互补关联加入稀疏有向图。检索时,CABLE 沿这些链接扩展宿主系统已检索到的种子,浮现出隐性的支撑证据。

Result: 用 Qwen3.5-27B、DeepSeek-chat 和 GPT-4o-mini 在 LoCoMo 与 MA-LongMemEval 上评测:与 A-MEM 结合的 CABLE 在全部系统级设置中均获得更高的平均 LLM-judge 分数;在证据分散于多条记忆或多个会话的场景(如开放域、多会话、偏好导向问题)上增益最大。CABLE 还成功集成到 SimpleMem 与 Mem0g 中。

Conclusion: 应优先构建稀疏的、与推理相关的、互补于宿主检索器的关联,而非复制其已有能力的密集语义链接;CABLE 证明了该策略能系统性提升长期记忆系统中的证据可达性与回答质量。

Abstract: As LLM agents operate across structured workflows and sessions, preserving long-term history does not ensure that later contexts can recover relevant evidence through a bounded memory interface. We study this evidence-reachability problem in long-term conversational memory, where retrieval still relies heavily on semantic similarity. This works well for topical recall, but it often misses earlier experiences, plans, or motivations that are semantically distant from the later events they help explain. Existing memory graphs provide cross-memory structure, yet links driven mainly by semantic overlap can duplicate what the host retriever already recovers. We argue that link construction should instead prioritize a sparse set of retriever-complementary associations. We present CABLE (Complementary Antecedent-Based Linking and Expansion), a plug-in augmentation that constructs links designed to extend the host retriever's direct semantic reach. For each new memory, CABLE generates antecedent-oriented queries, retrieves prior memories, subtracts candidates in the direct semantic neighborhood, and verifies the remainder before adding the accepted complementary associations into a sparse directed graph. At retrieval time, CABLE expands the host system's retrieved seeds along these links to surface implicit supporting evidence. We evaluate CABLE with A-MEM on LoCoMo and MA-LongMemEval, and further integrate it into SimpleMem and Mem0g on LoCoMo, using Qwen3.5-27B, DeepSeek-chat, and GPT-4o-mini. CABLE yields higher mean LLM-judge scores in every evaluated system-level setting, with the largest gains in categories where useful evidence is distributed across memories or sessions, including open-domain, multi-session, and preference-oriented questions. These results support prioritizing sparse, reasoning-relevant associations that complement rather than duplicate the host retriever.

</details>


### [129] [SpeechSense: A Paralinguistic-Focused Dataset for Fine-Grained Speech Sentiment Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17931)
*Shicheng Ma,Wenqian Cui,Irwin King*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 SpeechSense 数据集，通过 8 类人际立场分类与高保真语音合成及人工验证，证明声学线索对细粒度语音情感分析的关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语音情感分析存在两大局限：一是主流基于文本的流水线（ASR 级联文本分析）丢弃了韵律、语调等关键声学特征，无法捕捉声学含糊话语中的态度意义；二是现有基准在标签粒度上不匹配，偏重基本情绪（如开心、悲伤）而忽视社会敏感所需的人际立场（如自信、不耐烦）。

Method: 提出 SpeechSense 数据集：定义专门的 8 类人际立场分类体系（主要依赖韵律线索而非仅词汇内容），基于该体系构建经高保真语音合成和严格人工验证的精心整理数据集；并在多模态 LLM、纯文本 LLM 和语音编码器上开展综合实验对比。

Result: 实验表明具备声学访问能力的模型一致地优于纯文本基线，经验性地验证了声学线索在检测细微说话人态度中的首要地位，凸显了 SpeechSense 的必要性。

Conclusion: 细粒度语音情感分析需要超越文本的多模态声学信息，SpeechSense 为评估和推动该方向提供了有效基准，数据集与补充材料已公开。

Abstract: Recent advances in AI have revolutionized speech processing, yet effective speech understanding requires discerning not just what is said, but how it is said. Speech Sentiment Analysis plays a critical role in decoding these paralinguistic cues for diverse real-world applications such as recruitment and customer service. However, existing Speech Sentiment Analysis research faces two primary limitations. First, dominant approaches rely on text-centric pipelines that cascade Automatic Speech Recognition with text analysis. This process inevitably discards essential acoustic features like prosody and tone, failing to capture attitudinal meanings in acoustically ambiguous utterances. Second, current benchmarks suffer from a mismatch in label granularity, prioritizing basic emotions (e.g., happy, sad) over the nuanced interpersonal stances (e.g., confident, impatient) necessary for social sensitivity. To address these limitations, we propose a novel dataset, SpeechSense, for fine-grained speech sentiment analysis. Specifically, we define a specialized 8-class taxonomy of interpersonal stances detectable primarily through prosodic cues beyond lexical content alone. We then construct a curated dataset based on this taxonomy, built from high-fidelity speech synthesis and rigorous human validation. Comprehensive experiments across multi-modal LLMs, text-only LLMs, and speech encoders demonstrate that models with acoustic access consistently outperform text-only baselines. These results empirically validate the primacy of acoustic cues in detecting subtle speaker attitudes, highlighting the necessity of SpeechSense. Dataset and supplementary materials are available at https://github.com/Sher13cked/SpeechSense.

</details>


### [130] [Grading Needs a Rubric, Not Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17938)
*Jhen-Ke Lin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 小语言模型在依据明确评分量表（rubric）评分时，能以远低于大模型的成本达到同等可靠的开放式问答题评分效果；评分几乎完全取决于答卷本身，而与评分模型的智能水平无关，量表（尤其是其中的官方答案）是关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放式问答题评分成本高昂，且通常依赖昂贵的前沿大模型。作者希望验证一个设计原则：只要依据明确的评分量表，低成本小模型就能替代昂贵模型完成重复性评分工作，从而降低评分成本而不牺牲可靠性。

Method: 提出 any-to-bench 框架：由前沿模型在数据摄取时一次性阅读源文档，提取每道题及其评分量表；之后所有重复评分由低成本模型完成。实验选用两个模型家族、三种推理努力水平的六个经济型配置，对 24 道开放式问答题分别作答并各评分三次（共 3,456 个每题分数）；增设六个前沿级评委作为对照；并通过两组消融（去掉量表的准则与等级、进一步去掉官方答案）分解量表的作用。

Result: 成绩方差由答卷身份解释 95.6%，而评委身份仅解释 0.2%；提高答题者推理努力可使得分最多变化 0.143（满分为单位），而提高评分者推理努力最多仅使分数变化 0.006。六个前沿级评委复现了这些分数，且作为专家组并不比单个经济模型更可靠。消融显示：仅去掉量表的准则与等级但保留官方答案，结果无显著变化；进一步去掉官方答案后，信度（ICC）从 0.888 降至 0.628，分数被抬高，评委推理努力重新产生影响。实验未发现长度偏好或同家族偏好的证据。

Conclusion: 评分量表使得评分过程与评委智能解耦，而量表内起决定性作用的是官方答案。因此，基于量表的小语言模型评分是一种低成本的可靠方案，可用于大规模开放式问答评分。

Abstract: Small language models can grade open-ended examination answers as reliably as substantially more expensive models when they grade against an explicit rubric. We test this claim as the design principle behind any-to-bench: a frontier model reads source documents once, at ingestion, to extract each question and its rubric; lower-cost models then perform all repeated grading work. We evaluate six cost-efficient model configurations from two model families at three reasoning-effort levels. Each configuration answers 24 open-ended examination questions, and each also grades every answer sheet three times, yielding 3,456 per-question grades. Scores depend overwhelmingly on the answer being graded: answer identity explains 95.6% of score variance, whereas judge identity explains only 0.2%. Raising a writer's reasoning effort moves earned scores by as much as 0.143 of full marks, while raising a judge's reasoning effort moves assigned scores by at most 0.006. Six frontier-tier judges, added as a check, reproduce these scores and are no more reliable as a panel. Two ablations then decompose the rubric on the same questions and answers. Removing its criteria and levels while keeping the official answer changes nothing measurable. Removing the official answer as well collapses reliability (ICC 0.888 to 0.628), inflates scores, and makes judge reasoning effort matter again. The rubric is what decouples grading from judge intelligence, and within the rubric the official answer does nearly all the work. We find no evidence of length preference or same-family preference under rubric-anchored grading.

</details>


### [131] [Do Large Language Models Play Six Degrees of Separation? Measuring Topological Compression in Long-Context Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17950)
*Md. Faiyaz Abdullah Sayeedi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文绕过传统注意力权重，直接分析 LLM 隐藏状态流形的动态几何结构，证明深层潜在空间自发组织为“小世界网络”：深层推理层将远距语义锚点压缩为不超过 6 跳（“六度分离”）的可导航通路，而浅层句法层保持完全断裂。该框架还可用于零样本幻觉检测（RAGognize），事实性生成与源上下文保持约 3 跳结构完整性，幻觉则引发拓扑塌缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于注意力的可解释性方法因注意力汇聚（attention sinks）等路由伪影，无法真实刻画长上下文中的语义邻近关系；而 LLM 实现远距离多跳推理的内部机制仍不清楚。作者希望绕过注意力权重，直接从隐藏状态流形的几何/拓扑结构揭示深层推理的潜在机制。

Method: 将长上下文表示的连续相似度矩阵稀疏化为无权图，在两种不同架构上追踪高度不相交语义锚点之间的连通性；通过分析跨层的图拓扑（如小世界特性、语义跳数）刻画隐藏状态流形的动态几何结构，并将其应用于 RAG 场景下的零样本幻觉检测。

Result: 发现清晰的拓扑相变：早期句法层完全断裂，深层推理层将庞大概念距离突然压缩为高度可导航的通路，且严格受“六度分离”约束（语义跳数 ≤ 6）；在 RAGognize 数据集上，事实性生成与源上下文保持约 3 跳的结构完整性，而幻觉生成引发严重拓扑塌缩。

Conclusion: 该工作在数学上形式化了 Transformer 执行抽象推理的方式，并提出一种全新的、严格几何化的事实可靠性签名，为评估生成内容的真实性与可解释性提供了新工具。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) demonstrate remarkable multi-hop reasoning capabilities over long contexts, yet the internal mechanisms enabling these distant cognitive leaps remain poorly understood. Traditional attention-based interpretability often fails to capture true semantic proximity due to routing artifacts like attention sinks. In this paper, we bypass attention weights to directly analyze the dynamic geometry of the hidden state manifold, proving that deep LLM latent spaces natively organize into Small-World networks. By sparsifying the continuous similarity matrices of long-context representations into unweighted graphs, we trace the connectivity between highly disjoint semantic anchors across two distinct architectures. Our findings reveal a sharp topological phase transition: while early syntactic layers remain entirely fractured, deep reasoning layers abruptly compress massive conceptual distances into highly navigable pathways strictly bounded by the "Six Degrees of Separation" limit (=< 6 semantic hops). Furthermore, we demonstrate the practical efficacy of this framework by applying it to zero-shot hallucination detection within Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) using the RAGognize dataset. We show that factually grounded generations maintain structural integrity with their source context (approximately 3 hops), whereas hallucinations induce severe topological collapse. Ultimately, this work mathematically formalizes how transformers execute abstract reasoning and provides a novel, strictly geometric signature for evaluating factual reliability.

</details>


### [132] [When Writing Style Drifts: Benchmarking Authorship Verification under Distribution Shifts in Genre, Time and the AI-Era](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17979)
*Lotta Kiefer,Brisca Balthes,Christoph Leiter,Yamen Ajjour,Elena Schmidt,Steffen Eger*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 AVShift——首个用于系统评估多种分布偏移下作者身份验证（AV）的德语基准，包含超过 15 万对文本、覆盖 3 种体裁与 21 年时间跨度，系统评估了跨体裁、时间漂移和 AI 时代偏移的影响。实验表明微调 LLM 泛化最好，时间漂移是最强影响因素，而未发现可测量的 AI 时代偏移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者身份验证通常假设作者的写作风格足够稳定，但该假设在实践中受到体裁、时间变化及 AI 辅助写作等分布偏移的挑战。现有 AV 基准通常孤立研究这些因素且以英语为主，难以评估模型在现实条件下的鲁棒性，因此需要统一的、多偏移的评估框架。

Method: 构建 AVShift 基准：包含超过 15 万对文本，跨越 3 种体裁和 21 年，实现对跨体裁、时间与 AI 时代偏移的统一受控评估。在统一框架下对代表性方法进行基准确认：基于特征的方法、基于嵌入的方法和基于大语言模型（LLM）的方法，并进行跨偏移评估与文体特征稳定性分析。

Result: 微调后的 LLM 在跨体裁场景下泛化最佳，并能从风格多样的训练数据中显著获益；时间漂移是影响 AV 的最强因素之一，文档间时间间隔增大时性能显著下降；在 AVShift 中未发现可测量的 AI 时代分布偏移证据；文体特征分析揭示了跨体裁保持稳定的特征，但其相对重要性随具体体裁转换而变化。

Conclusion: AVShift 作为首个德语多分布偏移 AV 基准，揭示了微调 LLM 的优势、时间漂移的关键影响以及 AI 时代偏移的缺失证据，并指出文体特征的稳定性与体裁相关性。作者公开 AVShift 与代码以支持未来研究。

Abstract: Authorship verification (AV) assumes that an author's writing style remains sufficiently stable to distinguish it from that of other writers. In practice, however, this assumption is challenged by distribution shifts caused by changes in genre, time, and AI-assisted writing. Existing AV benchmarks typically study these factors in isolation and focus predominantly on English, limiting our understanding of model robustness under realistic conditions. We introduce AVShift, the first German benchmark for systematically evaluating AV under multiple distribution shifts. AVShift comprises over 150,000 text pairs spanning three genres and 21 years, enabling controlled evaluation of cross-genre, temporal, and AI-era shifts within a unified framework. We benchmark representative feature-based, embedding-based, and LLM-based approaches. Our experiments show that fine-tuned LLMs generalize best across genres and benefit substantially from stylistically diverse training data. We further demonstrate that temporal drift is one of the strongest factors affecting AV, with performance degrading significantly as the time gap between documents increases. In contrast, we find no evidence of a measurable AI-era distribution shift within AVShift. Finally, our feature analysis reveals stylistic features that remain stable across genres, while their relative importance varies depending on the specific genre transition. We release AVShift and our code for future research.

</details>


### [133] [Judge, Retrieve, or Abstain: Uncertainty-Guarded LLM Judging with Provable Risk Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17994)
*Sher Badshah,Ali Emami,Hassan Sajjad*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种风险受控的LLM评判框架，通过校准不确定性阈值并结合参数模式与检索增强模式的两级路由，在保证被接受裁决的误判率以高概率低于用户指定水平α的同时，显著提升覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无参考的LLM评判在客观任务中存在可靠性挑战：纯参数模式可能幻觉或证据不足，而工具增强虽能补充证据却增加算力成本且缺乏何时、如何使用证据的可靠机制；且两者都无法对被接受裁决的风险提供形式化控制。

Method: 提出风险受控框架：在留出集上校准不确定性阈值，利用有限样本Clopper–Pearson区间保证被接受裁决的错误发现率（FDR）以高概率低于用户指定水平α。当参数模式置信度不足时，将实例路由至检索增强模式，由评判器收集网络证据并在第二个校准阈值下重新评估；两级路由的有限样本保证无需额外假设即可成立。

Result: 在开放域问答基准及不同规模评判器上的实验表明，该框架能在保持目标错误率的同时，获得远高于单模式基线的覆盖率。

Conclusion: 两级风险受控路由框架能够在提供形式化可靠性保证（FDR控制）的前提下，有效利用检索增强弥补参数模式的缺陷，实现错误率与覆盖率的良好平衡。

Abstract: Using LLMs as judges has become standard practice for evaluating model outputs at scale. This is particularly common for subjective, open-ended tasks such as assessing helpfulness or alignment, where no single reference answer exists. However, objective tasks introduce a distinct reliability challenge for reference-free LLM judging. In the absence of a reference answer, the judge evaluates factual correctness either through its parametric knowledge or through tool augmentation. Although the former enables efficient evaluation, the judge may hallucinate or lack sufficient evidence for its verdict. Conversely, tool augmentation can provide additional evidence but introduces extra computational cost and requires an appropriate mechanism to determine when and how that evidence should be used reliably. More importantly, neither approach alone provides formal control over the risk of accepted verdicts or guarantees their reliability at a specified level. We propose a risk-controlled framework that calibrates uncertainty thresholds on a held-out set so that the false discovery rate among accepted verdicts remains below a user-specified level~$α$ with high probability, using finite-sample Clopper--Pearson intervals. When the parametric mode is not sufficiently confident, the instance is routed to a retrieval-augmented mode, where the judge gathers web evidence and re-evaluates the instance under a second calibrated threshold. The finite-sample guarantee carries over to this two-threshold routing without additional assumptions. Across open-domain QA benchmarks and judges of varying scales, the framework maintains the target error rate while achieving substantially higher coverage than single-mode baselines.

</details>


### [134] [The IOL-AI Challenge: An Open Challenge towards Advancing Linguistic Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18011)
*Eduardo Sánchez,Rita Berrada,Dan-Mircea Mirea,Sara Rajaee,Alexander Piperski,Ana Meta Dolinar,Boris Iomdin,Andrey Nikulin,Mariya Shmatova,Marzieh Fadaee,Julia Kreutzer*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 IOL-AI 挑战赛，用国际语言学奥林匹克竞赛（IOL）2026 的未见题目评测大模型的"语言学推理"能力，通过自动评分与官方评委双重评估，发现前沿模型 Claude Opus 4.8 达到金牌水平，但受限资源系统仅处于末 5%；模型能力不随规模单调提升，自动评分与评委评分排序一致但会压缩分差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 LLM 推理研究集中在数学和代码等规则明确的领域，模型只需在给定规则下推理；而语言学谜题要求模型先自行发现底层系统（规则）再进行推理，是对"发现规则+在规则内推理"这一更通用推理能力的更严格检验，因此作者希望用它作为可泛化推理能力的基准。

Method: 举办开放科学的 IOL-AI 挑战赛，使用 IOL 2026 个人赛的未公开题目，对 46 支队伍提交的 731 份系统进行评测；采用自动评分，并首次由 IOL 官方评委按人类选手同一评分标准进行人工评阅。对受限资源系统施加严格算力预算（单张 T4，30 分钟）；另对 15 个无约束的前沿与开源模型进行基准测试，并通过解码策略和输出处理方式分析性能提升来源。

Result: Claude Opus 4.8 的评委得分相当于金牌水平，而提交评阅的两个受限资源系统仅处于选手末 5%。能力不由模型规模决定：14B 的提交系统优于两倍于其规模的模型，提升主要来自解码与输出处理而非模型容量。自动指标与评委排序完全一致，但会压缩分差——对弱系统高估约 13 分、对强系统低估。此外，前沿模型对部分题目语言可能有先验知识，但对解决语言学推理任务帮助不大。

Conclusion: 语言学推理是评估可泛化推理能力的强有力基准：它要求模型先发现规则再推理，且模型对语言的先验知识并不会显著帮助解题。IOL-AI 挑战赛表明规模并非能力关键，工程优化（解码、输出处理）反而更有效，并提示自动评分应与人类评委评分结合以校准分差。

Abstract: Reasoning in LLMs is overwhelmingly studied in domains that provide a model with rules: mathematics and code. Linguistic puzzles invert this: the solver must first discover the system before reasoning within it. We present the IOL-AI Challenge, an open-science competition run on the unseen problems of the International Linguistics Olympiad (IOL) 2026 Individual Contest, evaluated both automatically and, for the first time, by members of the official IOL Jury under the same rubrics applied to human contestants. The challenge drew 731 submissions from 46 teams under a strict compute budget (one T4, 30 mins). We additionally benchmark 15 unconstrained frontier and open models, with Claude Opus 4.8 earning a jury score equivalent to a gold medal, while both resource-constrained systems we submitted for jury grading scored in the range of the bottom 5% of contestants. Capability was not determined by scale: 14B submissions outperform models twice their size, and gains come from decoding and output-handling rather than model capacity. We also found that automatic metrics rank systems exactly as the jury does, but compress the scale, upscoring weak systems by ~13 points and understating strong ones. Our analysis shows that while frontier models might have prior knowledge about some of the problem languages, it does not significantly help them solve the linguistic reasoning tasks, leaving linguistic reasoning as a strong benchmarking proxy for generalizable reasoning skills.

</details>


### [135] [Chain-of-Experience for Continual LLM Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18027)
*Haoqin Tu,Yunhao Fang,Yizhong Wang,Cihang Xie,Shen Yan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出“经验链”（Chain-of-Experience, CoE）评测范式，让LLM在推理阶段通过与环境或自身的迭代互动积累经验，实现超越零样本推理的持续改进，并在数学、编程与知识领域验证了其效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统LLM评测只关注一次性零样本推理，忽略了模型在推理时通过与反馈互动而“边用边学”的持续改进能力，这与人从经验中持续学习的特性相背离，因此需要一种能刻画推理阶段迭代学习能力的评测范式。

Method: 作者提出CoE框架，让模型通过迭代交互积累经验轨迹，形成持续改进循环。实例化多种反馈机制，包括模型自我反馈与环境信号（如正确性判定、公共编程测试通过率）。在数学、编程、知识三大领域，使用GPT-5、Gemini-2.5 Pro、Claude-4.5 Sonnet等8个LLM进行评测，并设置无反馈基线及现有测试时策略进行对比。

Result: 迭代经验利用一致优于无反馈基线，仅靠自我反馈即可取得显著提升；整体性能提升5.6%，同时API成本降低19%。结合互补反馈通道（如模型信号与正确性信号）可带来额外增益，且CoE的每token准确率高于现有测试时策略。实验还发现模型基础能力与改进容量正相关，模型在弱反馈或伪反馈下仍具鲁棒性，不同反馈贡献于不同改进维度，大部分提升出现在迭代早期。

Conclusion: CoE揭示了LLM在推理阶段通过迭代经验持续改进的潜力，说明结合多样反馈的迭代互动能在不增加甚至降低成本的同时显著提升性能，为更贴近人类持续学习的LLM评测与部署提供了新方向。

Abstract: Humans continuously learn from experience, whereas conventional large language model (LLM) evaluations ignore the models' ability to improve through inference-time interaction. In this paper, we study how LLMs learn from iterative experience at test time, a setting we refer to as Chain-of-Experience (CoE), where models accumulate experiential traces through iterative interactions with self or environmental feedback to form a continual improvement loop beyond zero-shot inference. We instantiate CoE with diverse feedback mechanisms, including model self-feedback and environmental signals such as correctness or public coding test pass rates, and evaluate across math, coding, and knowledge domains using 8 LLMs, including GPT-5, Gemini-2.5 Pro, Claude-4.5 Sonnet. Our study shows that leveraging iterative experience consistently outperforms feedback-free baselines, achieving substantial gains with self feedback alone, alongside a 5.6% overall improvement and 19% lower API cost across tasks and models. We further show that combining complementary feedback channels (e.g., model and correctness signals) yields additional gains, and that CoE delivers higher accuracy per token than existing test-time strategies. We observe a positive correlation between LLM base ability and improvement capacity, and show that models remain robust under weak or spurious feedback, with different feedback contributing to distinct improvement aspects and most gains emerging early in the iterations.

</details>


### [136] [Language Has Two Parameters: Narrative-Induced Semantic Plasticity and Phase-Sensitive Interpretation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18041)
*Hollis Robbins*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文主张语言意义除"振幅"（共现强度）外，还存在第二个参数"相位"：一种带符号、持久、索引到个体与二元关系的关系性参数，它决定共同激活的义项如何组合，并可在义项完全在场时逆转其贡献。标准Transformer及以单义性为目标的解释路径因忽略相位而存在根本缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 批评当前基于语料统计的主流语义观只捕捉了"振幅"（词语共现/关联强度），而语料对全体作者求和、去索引化，删除了历史索引的相位信息；Transformer在冻结推理中没有显式的相位表示，且以单义性为目标的解释计划实际上是在对抗相位所承载的"共存"现象（典故、反讽、引用）。

Method: 提出一个理论框架：引入"相位"作为第二关系性参数（带符号、持久、索引到个体与二元关系），并区分弱版本（解释需要该参数）与强版本（量子演算以"相遇顺序"约束约束这些现象，而有符号经典模型做不到）；借用量子概率作为该参数的记号系统，但不断言大脑中存在量子过程。附带提出六个可检验的预测（被抑制义项是否仍活跃、相遇顺序是否改变短语作用、标记信号是否改变共享短语的解读、被给予历史的模型是被其改变还是仅被其告知）。

Result: 论文提出而非实证验证：核心结果是概念性的弱版本主张——解释需要第二关系性参数（相位）；强版本（量子演算存在超出有符号经典模型的约束力）尚未成立，因其依赖的"相遇顺序约束"尚未推导出来。六个预测作为可检验之处被列出但文中未报告实验结果。

Conclusion: 该理论呼吁构建一种"具有智能体索引、携带相位的语义状态"的语言模型架构。其可辩护的结论是弱版本：含义解释需要一个带符号、持久、索引到个体与二元关系的关系性参数（相位），量子概率只是其数学记号。

Abstract: Language has two parameters. Count how often words occur together and you estimate amplitude, the strength of association. Word embeddings and attention weights refine that count, which sums every writer in the corpus together. This paper claims a second parameter, phase, which signed weights learned from a corpus do not supply. Phase exists only between meanings: it determines how coactivated meanings combine, and it can reverse what a meaning contributes while that meaning stays fully present. A speaker can set phase in the signal through linguistic form; encounters install phase relations and history distributes them. Population averaging deletes history-indexed phase: agent-deindexed corpora identify the population marginal state and determine no individual or dyadic state, at any scale. The standard transformer has no explicit representation for phase in frozen inference, and the interpretability program measuring progress by monosemanticity is optimizing against it: the coexistence it treats as a defect is the condition of allusion, irony, and quotation. Six predictions test whether a suppressed meaning stays active, whether encounter order changes what a phrase does, whether marking the signal changes how a shared phrase is taken, and whether a model given a history is changed by it or only informed about it. The claim defended is the weak version: interpretation requires a second relational parameter, signed, persistent, and indexed to individuals and dyads. Quantum probability is one notation for the parameter; nothing in the formalism claims quantum processes in the brain. The strong version, that the quantum calculus constrains these phenomena as signed classical models do not, rests on an encounter-order constraint not yet derived. The architecture the theory calls for is a language model with agent-indexed, phase-bearing semantic states.

</details>


### [137] [TokEval: A Tokenizer Evaluation Suite](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18062)
*Clara Meister*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 TokEval——一套超越传统指标（fertility、压缩率）的分词器评估框架，度量语言学和结构上有意义的属性（如 UTF-8 字符边界完整性、数字位值边界对齐），并通过受控预训练实验验证这些指标对下游性能的预测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型分词器通常在缺乏系统评估的情况下被选定，但其设计选择直接影响模型能力；其根本原因在于对"哪些分词器属性影响哪些下游性能"理解有限，因而需要可预测下游表现的内在度量标准来替代昂贵的预训练扫描。

Method: 提出 TokEval 框架，定义一组超越 fertility 与压缩率的分词器评估指标，涵盖信息论属性及结构敏感属性（如 UTF-8 字符边界完整性、数字位值边界对齐、换行符处理）。为验证指标的有效性，仅改变分词器的训练数据混合、预分词策略与训练算法，开展受控语言模型预训练实验，并在 bits-per-byte（分词器无关的困惑度）及语言理解、数学推理、代码生成等多个基准上评估模型。

Result: 实验表明不同内在属性对模型能力影响各异：信息论指标可预测语言建模能力（Spearman rho 最高达 0.80），而结构敏感指标（如数字与换行符处理度量）则与任务准确率相关，说明 TokEval 指标具备对下游性能的预测力。

Conclusion: TokEval 有望实现更具原则性的分词器评估，在内在度量与下游表现相一致的场景下，以内禀测量取代预训练扫描，从而降低分词器选型的成本。

Abstract: Language model tokenizers are typically selected with minimal evaluation, despite the fact that their design choices directly impact model capabilities. This can be partly attributed to a limited understanding of which tokenizer properties affect which aspects of downstream performance. We introduce TokEval, a framework of tokenizer evaluation metrics that goes beyond standard measures like fertility and compression rate to capture linguistically and structurally meaningful properties, e.g., UTF-8 character boundary integrity and digit place-value boundary alignment for mathematics. To validate whether these metrics are predictive of downstream model performance, we conduct controlled language model pretraining experiments, varying solely the tokenizers' training data mixture, pretokenization strategy, and training algorithm. We evaluate the resulting models on bits-per-byte (a tokenizer-agnostic version of perplexity) and several benchmarks, spanning linguistic understanding, mathematical reasoning, and code generation. Our experiments suggest that different intrinsic properties have different impacts on model abilities: information-theoretic metrics predict language modeling abilities (Spearman rho up to 0.80), while structure-sensitive metrics, such as those measuring digit and line-break handling, correlate with task accuracy. We hope TokEval enables more principled tokenizer evaluation, replacing pretraining sweeps with intrinsic measurement wherever the two agree.

</details>


### [138] [Multi-Agent AI System for Radiology Report Structuring and Quality Assurance with Independent Radiologist Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18072)
*Iryna Hartsock,Cesar Lam,Christopher Otteni,Aliya Qayyum,Robert Gatenby,Cyrillo Araujo,Ghulam Rasool*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文开发并评估了一个本地部署的多智能体AI系统，用于放射报告的结构化与质量保证（QA），在638份胸腹盆腔CT报告中完成句子级解剖结构划分，并对14.1%的报告标记出潜在问题，放射科医生评估显示其性能良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 放射报告标准化程度不足、结构不一，且传统质量保证依赖人工审核耗时费力。作者旨在通过本地部署的多智能体AI系统，在同一条工作流中同时实现报告结构化与质量保证，既提升报告规范性和可检索性，又降低数据隐私外泄风险。

Method: 回顾性纳入2023—2024年由15名认证放射科医生书写的638份胸部、腹部及盆腔CT报告。构建多智能体AI流水线：使用正则规则与本地大语言模型将报告在句子层面划分为标准解剖分段（保留原文内容），同时检测Findings与Impression段间或段内不一致、性别-解剖冲突，以及危急结果未记录沟通等问题。由两名认证放射科医生独立评估45份报告子集，评估重构正确性、信息遗漏/虚构情况及整体QA性能。

Result: 系统将全部报告的Findings段（22,270个句子）重构为预定义解剖格式且保留原文。共标记90份报告（14.1%），最常见为段间不一致（80份，12.5%）。放射科医生评估中，两名审阅者一致认为31份（69%）重构正确、2份（4%）重构错误，其余12份（27%）意见不一致；双方一致认为无临床重要信息遗漏、无伪造内容；84%的报告整体QA性能被评为“优秀”或“良好”，其余为“一般”。

Conclusion: 本地部署的多智能体AI系统可在单一工作流内同时完成放射报告结构化与质量保证，并在放射科医生评估中表现出良好性能，有望支持放射实践中的报告标准化与质量保证。

Abstract: Purpose: To develop and evaluate a locally deployed multi-agent AI system for radiology report structuring and quality assurance. Materials and Methods: This retrospective study included 638 radiology reports from CT examinations of the chest, abdomen, and pelvis dictated by 15 board-certified radiologists in 2023 and 2024. A multi-agent AI pipeline was developed to perform report structuring and quality assurance (QA). The system structured the report into standardized anatomical sections at the sentence level using regex rules and local large language models. It also detected mismatches between the Findings and Impression sections, or within sections; gender-anatomy conflicts; and undocumented communication of critical findings. Two board-certified radiologists independently evaluated a 45-report subset. Results: The multi-agent system structured the Findings sections of all reports (22,270 sentences) into a predefined anatomical format while retaining the original report content. The system flagged 90 (14.1%) reports, most commonly for section mismatches (80 reports, 12.5%). In the radiologist evaluation, both reviewers agreed that 31 (69%) were correctly restructured, 2 reports (4%) were incorrectly restructured, and disagreed on the remaining 12 reports (27%). Both reviewers agreed that no clinically important information was omitted and no fabricated content was introduced. Overall QA performance was rated as "excellent" or "good" in 84% of the evaluated reports, with the remaining reports rated as "fair". Conclusion: A locally deployed multi-agent AI system combined radiology report structuring and quality assurance within a single workflow. The system demonstrated favorable performance in radiologist evaluation. Such systems may support standardization of reporting and quality assurance in radiology practice.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [139] [Diagonal Multi-omics Integration of Heterogenous Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16968)
*Maksim V. Kukushkin,Mikhail S. Arbatskiy,Dmitriy E. Balandin,Alexey V. Churov*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究对角多组学异构数据集整合方法，通过分析耦合拉普拉斯算子在嵌入复欧氏空间的Stiefel流形同胚集合上的极值迹问题，提出基于梯度上升法的最大化方案，并以极大值与极小值点之差的范数作为数据集异质性的新度量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多组学数据整合面临生物学异质性带来的挑战，现有方法对数据异质性本质的理解不足，亟需从数学角度阐明异质性产生的差异，并设计可量化的异质性表征手段。

Method: 将问题建模为耦合拉普拉斯算子在同胚于Stiefel流形（嵌入复欧氏空间）的集合上的极值迹问题；在泛函分析框架下详细构造梯度上升法求解该最大化问题；进而在求得的极大、极小点基础上，利用两点之差的范数定义数据集异质性特征。

Result: 给出梯度上升法的泛函分析形式推导；引入一个以极值点之差范数度量的新型数据集异质性特征，用于对角多组学异构数据的整合分析。

Conclusion: 通过极值迹问题的数学化处理与梯度上升法求解，能够更清晰地刻画生物学异质性所产生的差异，所提出的异质性范数度量可作为多组学数据整合的有效分析工具。

Abstract: In this paper, we consider methods for the diagonal multi-omics integration of heterogeneous datasets. Several approaches to the nature of biological heterogeneity are analyzed and developed to comprehend more clearly the generated differences. Specifically, the extremal trace problems for the coupled Laplacian on sets homeomorphic to the Stiefel manifold embedded in the complex Euclidean space are investigated. The gradient ascent method for the maximization problem is elaborated in the classical terms of functional analysis, which is of significant interest in itself. On this basis, we introduce a novel characteristic of dataset heterogeneity by employing the norm of the difference between the maximum and minimum points.

</details>


### [140] [Policy Optimization and Statistical Inference for Online Contextual Matrix Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17173)
*Liner Xiang,Yixin Wang,Hengrui Cai*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出"在线情境矩阵博弈"框架，将情境信息融入多玩家在线博弈，并设计 OnGameLearn 算法同时兼顾玩家动作与情境的探索-利用权衡，提供了完整的统计保证（尾部界、纳什均衡收敛、渐近正态性、次线性遗憾），并给出策略价值的双重稳健 √T 一致估计，在模拟与真实酒店定价数据中验证有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的在线决策方法只能部分解决动态情境与策略交互并存的问题：情境老虎机只处理单智能体决策、忽略多玩家交互；在线矩阵博弈虽能刻画策略行为但假设支付固定、忽略情境信息。然而现实中（如竞争性酒店定价）支付会随情境信号演化，亟需一个能同时整合情境信息与多玩家策略交互的统一框架。

Method: 作者引入"在线情境矩阵博弈"框架整合情境信息与多玩家在线博弈，并提出 OnGameLearn 在线学习算法，在玩家动作与情境两个维度上高效平衡探索与利用。文章建立多类统计保证：支付矩阵估计的尾部界、估计纳什均衡的收敛性、参数估计量的渐近正态性以及次线性遗憾界。此外定义了矩阵博弈中的"策略价值"概念，并构造了双重稳健、√T 一致的估计量。

Result: OnGameLearn 获得了支付矩阵估计的尾部界、纳什均衡收敛性、参数估计渐近正态性与次线性遗憾等理论保证；策略价值估计量具备双重稳健性与 √T 一致性。模拟研究与真实酒店定价应用均表明，该算法能有效应对策略性与情境性相互交织的决策难题。

Conclusion: 论文填补了情境信息与多玩家策略博弈结合的理论空白，所提 OnGameLearn 算法兼顾动作与情境的探索-利用，具备完整统计保证，并在实证中表现良好，为动态情境下的竞争性定价等场景提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Online decision making often requires navigating a landscape shaped by both dynamic contexts and strategic interactions. In competitive pricing, for example, hotels must account for both dynamic contextual factors and rivals' strategic responses. Existing approaches address only part of this challenge: contextual bandits optimize single-agent decisions using observable features but ignore multi-player interactions, while online matrix games capture strategic behavior through Nash equilibrium but assume fixed payoffs, ignoring contextual information. How should agents act then when strategic payoffs evolve with contextual signals? We introduce \emph{online contextual matrix games} to integrate contextual information into multi-player online games. We further propose \emph{OnGameLearn}, an online learning algorithm that efficiently balances exploration and exploitation across both player actions and contexts. This approach comes with statistical guarantees: tail bounds for the estimated payoff matrix, the convergence of the estimated Nash equilibrium, the asymptotic normality of the parameter estimators, and the sublinear regret bound. We also develop the notion of \emph{policy value} in matrix games and develop a doubly robust, $\sqrt{T}$-consistent estimator for it. Across simulated studies and a real-world hotel pricing application, we find that OnGameLearn effectively navigates the intertwined challenges of strategic and contextual decision-making.

</details>


### [141] [Expressivity In Multimodal Contrastive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17203)
*Andrew Stuart,Florian Wolf*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文从总体密度估计视角研究对比学习架构的表达能力，证明双模态双塔CLIP是通用逼近器，而多模态的成对求和损失CLIP无法表示任意联合分布，并提出Hadamard-CLIP恢复通用逼近能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管对比学习（尤其是CLIP风格模型）在文本-图像生成、视觉-语言模型及多模态检索中取得巨大成功，其架构的表达能力仍缺乏理论理解。作者希望厘清一个纯表示容量问题：给定对比学习参数化族能逼近哪些联合分布。

Method: 采用总体层面的密度估计观点，将每种架构视为一个可由参数逼近多模态联合分布的参数化密度族。对双塔CLIP与成对相似度求和损失的多模态CLIP变体进行表达能力分析，并构造性地提出Hadamard-CLIP（在现有编码器之上增加一个可学习权重向量）。

Result: 表达能力强烈依赖于架构：双模态双塔CLIP是通用逼近器；而基于成对相似度求和的自然多模态推广（三模态及以上）可证明无法表示任意联合分布，但足以匹配所有成对条件分布；Hadamard-CLIP则能恢复任意模态数下对联合分布的通用逼近。

Conclusion: 对比学习的表达能力具有架构依赖性；成对求和的多模态CLIP存在表达能力缺口，而Hadamard-CLIP通过仅增加一个可学习权重向量，既恢复了联合分布的通用逼近能力，又保持了CLIP快速、可预计算嵌入的检索特性。

Abstract: Contrastive learning has become a cornerstone of modern representation learning, powering CLIP-style models that underpin text-to-image generation, vision-language models, and retrieval across a rapidly growing range of modalities. Despite this empirical success, the expressive power of these architectures remains poorly understood. To gain insight, we study expressivity by adopting a population-level, density-estimation viewpoint: each architecture comprises a parameterized set of densities whose parameters may be chosen to approximate the joint distribution of the modalities. This isolates a question of pure representational capacity: which joint distributions can a given contrastive family of parameterizations approximate to arbitrary accuracy? We show that expressivity is sharply architecture-dependent. For two modalities, the simple two-tower CLIP architecture is a universal approximator. A natural generalization of CLIP, widely used in practice when three or more modalities are present, is based on a loss found by summing over all pairwise similarities. This provably cannot represent arbitrary joint distributions, although we prove that it remains expressive enough to match all pairwise conditionals. Motivated by this gap, we propose Hadamard-CLIP, which adds a single learned weight vector on top of the existing encoders and restores universal approximation of the joint for any number of modalities while preserving CLIP's fast, precomputable-embedding retrieval.

</details>


### [142] [SPACE: Sample-cloud Predictive Adaptive Conformal Ellipsoids for Multivariate Time-Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17333)
*Baishi Li,Kelvin J. L. Koa,Ke-Wei Huang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: SPACE 是一种面向生成样本型多元概率预测器的共形包装器，它直接利用当前预测样本云估计时间局部的协方差几何，构建椭球联合预测区域，并通过动态后向窗口选择校准半径，从而在分布漂移下更贴近名义覆盖率并兼顾效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代概率时间序列预测器通过预测样本表达不确定性，但常用经验分位数得到的预测区域缺乏形式化覆盖保证，且在分布漂移下常偏离名义目标。现有多元共形方法虽可在线校准，却依赖历史残差并以固定或累计的回看窗口估计几何，既无法利用当前预测的瞬时依赖结构，又易受陈旧区制污染的干扰。

Method: 提出 SPACE 共形包装器：从当前预测样本云直接估计时间局部协方差几何以构建椭球联合预测区域，并通过动态后向窗口选择方案校准区域半径，从而将样本型多元预测器输出的经验预测集合转化为具有覆盖校准的联合区域。

Result: 在多样的多元数据集、概率预测器和共形基线组合上，SPACE 始终将实现的联合覆盖率和滚动覆盖率拉近名义目标，并相比竞争性包装器取得更优的覆盖率-效率权衡。

Conclusion: SPACE 通过即时利用当前预测的协方差结构并动态选择校准窗口，有效缓解了分布漂移与陈旧区制污染问题，为样本生成型多元预测器提供了一种覆盖更准且更高效的共形校准方案。

Abstract: Modern probabilistic time-series forecasters often express uncertainty through forecast samples. While typically converted into nominal prediction regions using empirical quantiles, these model-implied sets lack formal coverage guarantees and frequently deviate from nominal targets under distribution shift. Existing multivariate conformal methods can calibrate these regions online, but they typically estimate geometry from historical residuals using fixed or accumulating look-back windows. This reliance on the past limits their ability to exploit the instantaneous dependence structure of current predictions and leaves them vulnerable to stale-regime contamination. To address this, we propose SPACE, a conformal wrapper for sample-generating multivariate forecasters. SPACE constructs ellipsoidal joint prediction regions by estimating time-local covariance geometry directly from the current forecast sample cloud, calibrating the region's radius via a dynamic backward window-selection scheme. Across diverse multivariate datasets, probabilistic forecasters, and conformal baselines, SPACE consistently brings realized joint and rolling coverage closer to the nominal target, achieving superior coverage-efficiency tradeoffs relative to competing wrappers.

</details>


### [143] [Nonlocal Transition Kernel for Efficient Learning of Restricted Boltzmann Machines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17450)
*Kaiji Sekimoto,Muneki Yasuda*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文针对受限玻尔兹曼机（RBM）学习中因精确期望难以计算而依赖采样的难题，提出了一种基于深度回火（DT）RBM序列上的往返（round-trip）转移核，可在单次转移内实现非局部移动，提升采样质量与学习稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RBM学习需要计算难以精确求解的期望，通常采用分块吉布斯采样（BGS）近似，但BGS的局部性在高能垒情形下采样质量差，影响学习性能。深度回火（DT）虽能缓解局部性问题，但需要通过RBM序列多步移动才能实现非局部转移，算法开销大。因此需要一种能在单次转移内实现非局部移动的转移核。

Method: 本文在DT所用的可学习RBM序列（含训练RBM）上定义了一种具有往返（round-trip）结构的转移核，该核在保持RBM序列不变（不变性）的前提下，可在单次转移内完成非局部移动。

Result: 数值实验表明，与BGS和DT相比，所提转移核能更频繁地进行非局部转移，在更少的转移次数下获得更高的采样质量；基于该核的学习更稳定，并缓解了BGS和DT学习中出现的训练失败问题。

Conclusion: 所提出的往返转移核有效克服了BGS的局部性与DT的多步开销问题，提升了RBM的采样质量和学习稳定性，为RBM训练提供了一种更高效、更稳健的采样方案。

Abstract: Learning restricted Boltzmann machines (RBMs) is computationally challenging because it requires expectations whose exact evaluation is generally intractable. The expectations are typically evaluated using a sampling approximation based on blocked Gibbs sampling (BGS), which is a local Markov chain Monte Carlo transition kernel. However, the locality of BGS can lead to poor sampling quality when the RBM has high energy barriers, thereby degrading learning performance. Deep tempering (DT), which performs parallel tempering over a sequence of learnable RBMs including the training RBM, alleviates this locality issue. However, DT algorithmically requires multiple steps to move through the RBM sequence to achieve a nonlocal transition. In this paper, we propose a transition kernel defined over the RBM sequence used in DT. The proposed kernel has a round-trip structure over the sequence, enabling nonlocal moves within a single transition while leaving the RBM sequence invariant. Numerical experiments show that the proposed kernel performs nonlocal transitions more frequently and achieves higher sampling quality with fewer transitions than BGS and DT. We further verify that learning based on the proposed kernel is more stable and mitigates the training failures observed with BGS- and DT-based learning.

</details>


### [144] [Online Generalized Sparse Regression: How Does Overparametrization Help?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17466)
*Shuoguang Yang,Qiang Sun*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出一种在线广义稀疏约束回归框架，涵盖在线基数约束线性回归与低秩矩阵感知，通过高效的在线硬阈值算法实现闭式更新和仅存储摘要统计量，并在适当过参数化下达到最优统计速率与全局收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线正则化稀疏回归面临四大挑战：正则化参数无法在每一轮在线动态更新、存储与内存复杂度高、难以通过闭式更新实现实时计算、以及难以在现实假设下获得最优统计保证。作者希望通过约束式（而非正则化式）的稀疏建模来规避动态调参问题，从而更好地解决在线稀疏回归。

Method: 提出在线广义稀疏约束回归框架，具体针对在线基数约束线性回归和低秩矩阵感知。设计了一种高效的在线硬阈值算法：利用闭式更新（而非每轮求解完整优化问题）实现实时计算，并仅需存储摘要统计量以降低计算、内存与存储开销。理论分析表明，在投影集适当过参数化的条件下，尽管问题具有非凸性和组合性质，算法仍能以最优统计速率实现全局收敛。

Result: 数值实验表明，所提方法在最先进（state-of-the-art）的替代方法中表现一致地更优；理论层面证明在适当过参数化下算法达到最优统计速率的全局收敛。

Conclusion: 通过将在线稀疏回归重新表述为约束优化问题，本文克服了在线正则化方法中动态调参等固有困难，提出的在线硬阈值算法兼具计算、内存和存储效率，并在理论与实验上均取得优越性能。

Abstract: Regularized sparse regression has been extensively studied in the offline setting, but online formulation remains relatively under-explored. This gap stems from four key challenges: (i) the infeasibility of dynamically updating the regularization parameter in every online round, (ii) managing storage and memory complexity, (iii) enabling real-time computation via closed-form updates rather than solving full optimization problems at each round, and (iv) achieving optimal statistical guarantees under realistic assumptions. In this paper, we propose an online generalized-sparsity-constrained regression framework, focusing on online cardinality-constrained linear regression and low-rank matrix sensing. Unlike online regularized regression, our constrained formulation eliminates the need for dynamic parameter tuning. We introduce an efficient online hard-thresholding algorithm that performs closed-form updates and requires storing only summary statistics, making it computationally, memory, and storage efficient. Despite the inherent nonconvexity and combinatorial nature of the formulation, our algorithm achieves global convergence at the optimal statistical rate under realistic assumptions, provided that the projection set is properly overparameterized. Numerical experiments demonstrate that our method consistently outperforms state-of-the-art alternatives.

</details>


### [145] [Feature Priming in Online Linear Regression: Sparse-Regret Lower Bounds and a Tight Univariate Rate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17573)
*Huibo Xu,Shi Fu,Qixin Zhang,Dacheng Tao*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文对 COLT 2023 的开放问题给出否定答案：三种 feature priming 规则均无法在高维在线预测中获得稀疏对数级后悔界，其下界为 Ω(min{T,√d})；同时证明后悔受数据秩控制，并给出了匹配上界的构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维在线预测中，最优预测器可能仅依赖少数特征，理想的后悔应随稀疏性而非环境维数缩放。Feature priming 通过估计历史特征权重并在重缩放设计上重拟最小范数预测器来实现这一目标。Warmuth 与 Amid 在 COLT 2023 提出开放问题：三种此类规则中是否有任何一种能获得有竞争力的在线后悔保证。本文旨在解决该问题。

Method: 作者采用基于过去数据的自然 Moore–Penrose 协议，识别出一个共同障碍——“廉价干扰插值”(cheap nuisance interpolation) 使重拟过程低估真正有预测力的坐标。通过精确的目标质量恒等式(target-mass identity)和双符号(two-sign)论证，将该效应转化为截断预测损失(clipped prediction loss)。利用 Hadamard 构造证明三种规则在面对零损失一稀疏比较器时均产生 Ω(min{T,√d}) 后悔，并推广到固定素数幂及规则间的选择器情形。

Result: 三种 feature priming 规则均不能实现稀疏对数级后悔，下界为 Ω(min{T,√d})。反向地，后悔受数据秩(data rank)控制；欧氏归一化三角构造在 powered 单变量 priming 下（即便加入非负二阶段岭正则）匹配该依赖关系；配对的岭构造亦覆盖全部三种 powered 规则。冻结语言模型激活上的探索性诊断展现出干扰插值、目标权重与损失之间同样的关系。

Conclusion: 对 COLT 2023 开放问题给出否定结论：基于过去数据的三种 feature priming 规则均无法获得稀疏对数级在线后悔保证，存在 Ω(min{T,√d}) 的通用下界；后悔受数据秩而非环境维数控制，并可被特定构造匹配。精确的多元与 Pearson 前沿仍是开放问题。

Abstract: In high-dimensional online prediction, the best predictor may depend on only a few features, so regret should scale with sparsity rather than the ambient dimension. Feature priming pursues this goal by estimating feature weights from past data and refitting a minimum-norm predictor on the rescaled design. Warmuth and Amid asked at COLT 2023 whether any of three such rules admits a competitive online regret guarantee. Using the natural Moore--Penrose protocol based only on past data, we give a negative answer to the sparse-logarithmic form of this COLT open problem. Our analysis identifies a common obstruction: cheap nuisance interpolation causes the refit to underweight the truly predictive coordinate. An exact target-mass identity and a two-sign argument turn this effect into clipped prediction loss. Hadamard constructions force $Ω(\min\{T,\sqrt{d}\})$ regret for all three rules against a zero-loss one-sparse comparator, with extensions to fixed prime powers and selectors among the rules. Conversely, regret is controlled by data rank, and a Euclidean-normalized triangular construction matches this dependence for powered univariate priming, even under nonnegative second-stage ridge regularization; a paired ridge construction also covers all three powered rules. Exploratory diagnostics on frozen language-model activations exhibit the same relation among nuisance interpolation, target weight, and loss. The exact multivariate and Pearson frontiers remain open.

</details>


### [146] [Toward the Optimal Regret-Instability Trade-off in Multi-Armed Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17841)
*Kaifei Wang,Yinyu Ye,Han Zhong*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究多臂老虎机算法中后悔(regret)与不稳定性(instability)之间的权衡，证明了有限时间下界 R·S ≥ C·T^(3/2)，并提出 SLE-UCB 算法使其乘积达到 O(T^(3/2) log K)，在 T 上精确匹配下界、在 K 上仅差对数因子，从而解决文献中关于"臂相关后悔-不稳定性前沿"的开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多臂老虎机算法通常仅以后悔为评价指标，但相近的后悔值可能对应完全不同的臂选择分配。这种"不稳定性"（即各轮独立运行中终末拉臂次数的标准差）在实际应用中至关重要，却长期缺乏严格的有限时间刻画。作者旨在厘清最坏后悔与不稳定性之间的精确权衡关系，并回应文献中提出的关于臂依赖的"后悔-不稳定性前沿"的开放问题。

Method: 作者定义了不稳定性 S_{K,T} 为 K 个臂、T 轮下终末拉臂次数的最大标准差。首先在有限时间后悔条件下、且不依赖先验渐近分析所需的正则性假设，证明下界 R_{K,T}·S_{K,T} ≥ C·T^(3/2)。接着提出可调优的 Sle-UCB 算法，它结合"运行下包络指数(running lower-envelope index)"与"递减拉臂计数稳定器(decreasing pull-count stabilizer)"。为证明不稳定性上界，作者开发了一种新的"离线前缀表示(offline top-prefix representation)"，消除了在线决策的路径依赖性，并结合单奖励扰动与 Efron-Stein 不等式来控制拉臂次数的方差。

Result: Sle-UCB 满足 R_{K,T}·S_{K,T} = O(T^(3/2) log K)，其隐式常数与 K、T 无关：在 T 上精确匹配下界，在 K 上仅相差对数因子。后悔与不稳定性各自对 K 呈倒数依赖关系，而二者的乘积对 K 无多项式依赖。

Conclusion: 本文严格确立了多臂老虎机中后悔与不稳定性之间的基本权衡：其乘积下界为 T^(3/2) 量级，且 Sle-UCB 算法达到近乎最优的上界（在 K 上仅差对数因子）。这一结果解决了文献中关于臂依赖后悔-不稳定性前沿的开放问题，并提供了新的离线前缀表示分析工具。

Abstract: Multi-armed bandit algorithms are evaluated by regret, yet comparable regret can coexist with different allocations across independent runs. We study the trade-off between worst-case regret $\mathcal{R}_{K,T}$ and instability $\mathcal S_{K,T}$, defined as the largest standard deviation of a terminal pull count, for $K$ arms and $T$ rounds. We prove the finite-time lower bound $\mathcal R_{K,T}\mathcal S_{K,T}\ge C T^{3/2}$, where $C$ is independent of $K$ and $T$, under a finite-time regret condition and without the regularity assumptions imposed in the prior asymptotic analysis. We also introduce Stabilized Lower-Envelope UCB (\textup{\textsc{SLE-UCB}}), a new tunable algorithm combining a running lower-envelope index with a decreasing pull-count stabilizer. \textup{\textsc{SLE-UCB}} satisfies $\mathcal R_{K,T}\mathcal S_{K,T}=O(T^{3/2}\log K)$, with an implicit constant independent of $K$ and $T$, matching the lower bound exactly in $T$ and within a logarithmic factor in $K$. To prove the instability bound, we develop a new offline top-prefix representation that removes path dependence from online decisions. Together with single-reward perturbations and the Efron--Stein inequality, this representation controls pull-count variance. Thus, regret and instability depend reciprocally on $K$, while their product has no polynomial dependence on $K$. These results resolve the open question raised in the literature concerning the sharp arm-dependent regret--instability frontier.

</details>


### [147] [Where A Small Language Model Helps in Invoice Categorisation, Understood Through Embedding Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18033)
*Emma Ceccherini,Daniel Lawson,Anjulika Salhan*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究利用小语言模型（SLM）实现发票到总账（GL）科目的自动分类，分析预训练句子嵌入的几何结构，并证明微调后的SBERT在单GPU上即可达到0.96准确率，优于零样本大模型和供应商身份基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发票GL科目归类是一项需要专业会计判断的复杂任务，正确类别依赖于购买方业务性质、供应商和发票文本的微妙关系。虽然行业广泛采用AI自动化，但在内部构建基于小语言模型的方案可同时降低成本、提升数据安全、保密性和可解释性，因此作者探索这一技术路线的可行性与效果。

Method: 作者先分析SBERT（小型句级Transformer）和经典SLM DeBERTa的预训练嵌入几何结构；发现金融语料的句子嵌入空间全局各向异性但由局部各向同性簇组成，且这些簇与供应商身份强相关。随后在单GPU上微调SBERT进行发票分类，并与零样本LLM和基于供应商身份的基线模型进行对比；同时通过增加客户专属样本评估其对新客户的泛化能力（约100条样本即可达到0.9 F1），并结合几何分析探究预训练嵌入结构与分类性能的关联，以及结构化输入对模型的影响。

Result: SBERT微调后单GPU上发票分类准确率达0.96，高于零样本LLM和供应商身份基线，且在小型、困难类别和新客户上性能提升更明显。对新客户约100条发票即可达到0.9 F1。几何分析表明预训练嵌入几何与分类性能相关，并揭示一个反直觉发现：对人类读者有帮助的结构化输入并未提升SLM性能。

Conclusion: 在内部构建SLM实现发票GL分类是可行且有前景的方案，兼具成本、安全与可解释性优势。预训练嵌入的局部各向同性簇结构（与供应商强相关）与分类性能密切相关，同时结构化输入的无效性提示SLM与人类的感知和处理方式存在本质差异。

Abstract: Categorising invoices into the correct General Ledger (GL) code underpins financial reporting and tax compliance. This is a skilled accounting judgement rather than a routine task: the correct category depends subtly on the nature of the purchasing business, the vendor and the invoice text. Whilst AI is increasingly being adopted across industries to automate tasks, including invoice categorisation, implementations built on in-house small language models (SLMs) can simultaneously reduce cost and improve data security, confidentiality, and interpretability. We investigate this approach by first analysing the pre-trained embedding geometry of a small sentence transformer (SBERT) and classic SLM (DeBERTa). The sentence-embedding space of this financial corpus is globally anisotropic but composed of locally isotropic clusters, extending prior token-level findings to sentence embeddings in a financial setting, and these clusters are strongly correlated with the vendor identity. SBERT fine-tuned on a single GPU reaches 0.96 accuracy on invoice classification, above both a zero-shot LLM and a vendor identity baseline, increasing performance for smaller, challenging categories and new clients. For this important generalisation problem, SBERT reaches 0.9 F1 with roughly 100 client-specific invoices, showing that an in-house SLM implementation is promising. Combining these results with geometric analysis shows that pre-trained embedding geometry is associated with classification performance and reveals a counterintuitive finding that a structured input that would help a human reader does not improve the SLM performance.

</details>


<div id='stat.ME'></div>

# stat.ME [[Back]](#toc)

### [148] [Incorporating Animal Movement into Continuous-Time Spatial Capture-Recapture Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17046)
*Clara Panchaud,Ruth King,David Borchers,Hannah Worthington*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种将个体运动显式纳入空间标记-重捕（SCR）模型的连续时间框架，以纠正因忽略运动驱动的检测相关而导致的种群数量正偏差，并通过美国貂相机陷阱数据验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准空间标记-重捕（SCR）模型假设在给定活动中心后，各时点检测条件独立，未显式建模个体在检测事件之间的运动。当个体运动导致检测间存在依赖性时，该假设可能失效，进而使种群数量推断产生偏差。因此需要开发能显式整合运动的连续时间建模框架。

Method: 将个体运动建模为离散化景观上的连续时间马尔可夫链，检测事件视为依赖于个体状态的泊松过程；由此构建马尔可夫调制的标记泊松过程表示，使检测信息能够反映个体观测时刻的潜在位置，并在连续时间下实现基于似然的推断。

Result: 模拟研究表明，忽略运动驱动的检测依赖会导致种群数量的正偏估计，而所提模型能恢复无偏估计并提供空间利用的附加推断。对美国貂相机陷阱数据的应用展示了该框架在运动与密度推断上的新洞察。

Conclusion: 显式建模个体运动对于空间标记-重捕研究中的可靠推断至关重要，该连续时间框架能有效纠正偏差并丰富生态学推断。

Abstract: Estimation of wildlife population size and spatial dynamics is central to ecology and conservation. Spatial capture-recapture (SCR) models estimate abundance, using detections at sensors such as camera traps, by linking detection probability to the distance between detectors and latent individual activity centres. However, standard SCR formulations assume detections are conditionally independent over time given activity centres without explicitly modelling movement between detection events. This assumption can be problematic when individuals exhibit movement-driven dependence in detections, potentially leading to biased inference on population size. We address this important issue by developing a continuous-time framework that integrates movement into spatial capture-recapture. Individual movement is modelled as a continuous-time Markov chain over a discretised landscape, and detections arise as state-dependent Poisson events. This yields a Markov-modulated marked Poisson process representation, in which detections provide information about an individual's latent location at the time of observation and allow likelihood-based inference in continuous time. We show through simulation studies that ignoring movement-driven dependence can lead to positively biased estimates of population size, whereas the proposed model recovers unbiased estimates and provides additional inference on space use. An application to camera-trap data of American martens illustrates how the framework yields new insights into movement and density. These results demonstrate that explicitly modelling movement is critical for reliable inference in spatial capture-recapture studies.

</details>


### [149] [Identifying Model Quality Effects on User Engagement: A Within-Version Causal Estimator with Synthetic Data Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17187)
*John Tribbia*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种利用单一模型版本内能力非均匀提升来识别因果效应的新方法，将用户任务分布差异转化为"体验质量"的变异，从而估计模型更新对用户参与度的真实因果影响。合成数据验证显示原始估计量恢复81%–88%的真实效应，经测量误差校正后恢复至1.017，显著优于仅恢复62%–63%的朴素方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM团队面临的核心难题是：离线基准提升与上线后用户参与度上升同时出现，但难以把因果从相关中剥离。同时发生的营销、媒体报道和季节性需求会混淆信号，无法判断模型更新是否真正驱动了参与度增长。

Method: 提出一种利用单一模型版本内部各能力质量提升不均匀性的因果估计方法。由于不同能力（如编码大幅提升、写作小幅提升）改进幅度不一，用户因任务分布不同而体验到不同质量，这种体验质量的跨用户变异提供了因果识别信号。方法包含原始估计量、针对使用量测量误差的"变量含误差"去衰减校正（采用重测信度比，均值相关系数0.861），并通过置换检验、敏感性分析与种子敏感性测试进行稳健性验证。

Result: 在已知真实值的合成数据上，原始估计量恢复81%至88%的真实效应，剩余损失源于使用量估计的测量误差；经误差去衰减校正后恢复至1.017（自助法95%置信区间0.915–1.109）。相比之下，朴素方法分别仅恢复63%（无版本控制）和62%（使用实时而非冻结使用模式）。置换检验确认框架能区分真实因果效应与噪声；敏感性分析显示随着前置期从3周增至7周，恢复率从78%单调提升至87%，揭示样本时长与估计精度间的明确权衡；种子敏感性测试进一步确认结果在不同随机抽样下稳定。

Conclusion: 该因果估计框架能有效利用单一版本内能力非均匀提升带来的体验质量变异，从混淆信号中识别模型更新对用户参与度的真实因果效应，并在稳健性、灵敏度和误差校正方面均优于朴素方法。

Abstract: Every team building Large Language Models (LLMs) faces a core challenge: offline benchmarks show performance gains and user engagement rises post deployment, but isolating cause from effect remains difficult. Simultaneous marketing, media coverage, and seasonal demand obscure whether model updates truly drive engagement gains.
  This paper presents a novel causal estimation approach that leverages non uniform quality improvements across capabilities within a single model version. Because capabilities improve unevenly (e.g., strong gains in coding versus modest gains in writing), users experience varied quality depending on their task distribution. This variation in experienced quality provides causal signal for estimation.
  We validate this approach on synthetic data with known ground truth. The raw estimator recovers 81% to 88% of the true effect, with the remainder lost to measurement error in usage estimates. Applying an errors in variables disattenuation adjustment (using a test retest reliability ratio mean correlation of 0.861) corrects the estimate to 1.017 (bootstrapped 95% CI: 0.915 to 1.109).
  By contrast, naive methods fail significantly, recovering only 63% without version controls and 62% using real time rather than frozen usage patterns. Permutation tests confirm the framework distinguishes true causal effects from noise. Sensitivity analysis indicates recovery improves monotonically from 78% to 87% as pre period length increases from 3 to 7 weeks, highlighting a clear tradeoff between sample duration and estimator precision. Seed sensitivity tests further confirm stability across random draws.

</details>


### [150] [Nonparametric Estimation of Extropy, Rényi Extropy, and Tsallis Extropy: Almost Sure Convergence and Asymptotic Normality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17191)
*Amadou Diadie Ba*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文为非参数估计下有限离散随机变量的外熵（extropy）及其推广（α-Rényi 外熵与 α-Tsallis 外熵）提出了插件估计方法，建立其几乎处处收敛速率与渐近正态性，并通过仿真验证了理论结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 外熵及其推广（α-Rényi 与 α-Tsallis 外熵）作为不确定性度量在预测、风险评估与不确定决策等应用中有重要价值，但缺乏针对有限离散随机变量的严格非参数估计理论（收敛速率、渐近分布），这限制了其实际应用的理论基础。

Method: 采用非参数插件（plug-in）估计方法，基于经验分布构造外熵、α-Rényi 外熵与 α-Tsallis 外熵的估计量，并利用概率极限理论与渐近分析推导其几乎处处收敛速率和渐近正态性。

Result: 建立了插件估计量的几乎处处收敛速率与渐近正态性；通过全面的仿真研究验证了理论结果的有效性。

Conclusion: 该研究为外熵类度量在实际应用（预测、风险评估、不确定决策等）中的使用提供了坚实的理论基础。

Abstract: This paper proposes a nonparametric estimation procedure for extropy and its extensions, namely the α-Rényi and α-Tsallis extropies, for finite discrete random variables. We establish almost sure rates of convergence and asymptotic normality for the plug-in estimators. The theoretical results are validated through a comprehensive simulation study. The findings provide a solid foundation for the use of extropy-based measures in practical applications, including forecasting, risk assessment, and decision-making under uncertainty.

</details>


### [151] [SDR Variance Estimates in Small Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17353)
*Eric Slud,Tim Trudell*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文系统阐述了逐次差复制（SDR）方差估计方法，回顾了小域估计中SDR方差估计的偏差结果。研究表明，实现SDR所用的循环次数D在3到5之间即可，更大值几乎无关紧要；SDR会以系统化的方式膨胀小域平均估计方差，膨胀幅度通常中等（样本量为20的小域不超过15%），且SDR估计在小域中变异性极大，标准差往往远超任何偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SDR是大规模全国家庭调查（如美国社区调查、当前人口调查）的主要方差估计方法，但在其应用场景中通常缺乏第二种方差估计方法可供比较，因此只能依靠模拟研究其性能。本文旨在系统梳理SDR方法、总结已有偏差结果，并深入考察SDR在小域估计中的偏差与变异性表现，以填补对该方法认识上的空白。

Method: 本文首先对SDR方法进行详尽阐述，并回顾已发表的相关结果；随后通过理论公式推导与模拟研究，分析SDR实现中的循环次数D对小域偏差的影响，并考察小域中平均估计方差如何随连续枚举层内属性均值、方差及调查权重的模式而产生系统性膨胀。

Result: 实现SDR所需的循环次数D应不小于3以控制估计变异性，但无需大于5，超出该范围D的取值对小域偏差几乎无关紧要。理论与模拟均表明，SDR会膨胀小域平均估计方差，膨胀幅度随各层属性均值、方差和权重的模式系统性变化，总体中等（样本量为20的小域不超过15%），但在特殊情形下可能更大。此外，SDR估计在小域中变异性极大，其标准差往往远超任何偏差。

Conclusion: SDR作为复式多阶段调查（含最终系统抽样阶段）的主要方差估计方法，其性能受循环次数D影响有限，但需注意小域中方差膨胀与估计高变异性的问题。建议实践中D取3至5即可，同时应认识到SDR在小域估计中的内在局限。

Abstract: Successive Difference Replication (SDR) is a replication based method of variance estimation introduced by Fay and Train (1995) for estimators based on complex multistage surveys, especially those including a final systematic sampling stage. The method has been used for many years as the primary variance-estimation methodology in large national household surveys administered by the Census Bureau, including the American Community Survey and also the Current Population Survey's monthly estimates based on self-representing strata. In settings where it is applied, generally no second method of variance estimation has been available, so the performance of SDR has been studied via simulation by various authors, for variances of survey totals and of nonlinear survey estimators. This paper begins with a thorough exposition of the SDR method and review of previously published results on the small-domain biases of SDR variance estimation. It is shown that the number D of cycles used in implementing SDR should be 3 or larger, in order to control the variability of SDR estimates, but need not be larger than 5. Beyond that, the value of D is virtually irrelevant to the occurrence of small-domain bias in SDR. The SDR method is shown via theoretical formulas and simulation to inflate average estimated variances in small domains by amounts that vary systematically with the patterns of attribute means and variances and survey weights in consecutively enumerated strata. The degree of average variance inflation is generally moderate, no more than 15 percent in domains with sample size 20, but can be larger in special settings. Moreover, SDR estimates are extremely variable in small domains, with standard deviations often far larger than any biases.

</details>


### [152] [Modified Bryson-Frazier Smoothing and Hyperparameter Learning for Temporal Gaussian Process Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17595)
*Tom Colemont,Brecht Evens,Tjonnie G. F. Li,Frederik De Ceuster*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文重新引入修正的 Bryson-Frazier（MBF）平滑器，作为一维高斯过程回归状态空间模型中 RTS 平滑器的替代方案，以规避预测状态协方差矩阵求逆导致的数值不稳定，并统一支持推理与核超参数学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一维平稳可积核函数的高斯过程可以用状态空间表示并通过卡尔曼滤波与 RTS 平滑实现线性时间推理，但 RTS 平滑器需要对预测状态协方差矩阵求逆，矩阵可能病态从而引起数值不稳定，因此需要数值更稳健的替代方案。

Method: 重新审视修正的 Bryson-Frazier（MBF）平滑器，将其应用于高斯过程回归的状态空间表示，替代 RTS 平滑器执行后向平滑，并复用其计算得到的中间量来求负对数边际似然的梯度以进行核超参数学习。

Result: MBF 平滑器在降低计算成本与内存需求的同时，能得到与 RTS 平滑器相同的后验分布，且避免了病态协方差矩阵求逆及其数值不稳定；其产生的中间量可几乎零额外代价地复用于核超参数梯度计算。

Conclusion: MBF 平滑器为具有平稳可积核函数的一维高斯过程回归提供了一种统一且数值稳健的推理与核超参数学习方案，是一致且更优的 RTS 替代选择。

Abstract: One-dimensional Gaussian processes with stationary, integrable kernel functions admit exact or arbitrarily accurate state-space representations, enabling linear-time inference through Kalman filtering and Rauch-Tung-Striebel (RTS) smoothing. However, the RTS smoother requires inversion of predicted state covariance matrices, which can become ill-conditioned and may therefore lead to numerical instabilities. In this work, we revisit the modified Bryson-Frazier (MBF) smoother as an alternative to the RTS smoother for Gaussian process regression in its state-space representation. In addition to reducing computational cost and memory requirements, the MBF smoother computes the same posterior distributions as the RTS smoother while avoiding the problematic covariance matrix inversion and the associated numerical instabilities. Furthermore, we demonstrate that the intermediate quantities computed by the MBF smoother can be reused to compute gradients of the negative log marginal likelihood, enabling kernel hyperparameter learning with minimal additional cost. Together, these results establish the MBF smoother as a unified and numerically robust approach to inference and kernel hyperparameter learning for one-dimensional Gaussian process regression.

</details>


### [153] [Blinded sample size review for McNemar's test based on primary and surrogate endpoints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17784)
*Markus Schepers,Werner Brannath,Esther Hoffmann,Julia Stingl,Irene Schmidtmann*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文针对配对的二分类数据，发展了McNemar检验的盲态样本量再估计方法，并利用短期替代终点辅助再估计，在保持一类错误的同时达到目标功效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受一项儿童青光眼前瞻性随机临床试验的启发，需要在期中分析时基于配对二分类终点进行样本量再估计；且部分患者期中仅有短期替代终点可用，需利用替代终点信息作出调整。

Method: 推导给定期中主要终点时McNemar检验的条件功效表达式，据此建立样本量再估计规则；对仅有替代终点的情况，利用替代结局到主要结局的转移率，条件于所有可能的不一致对数，提出基于替代终点的再估计方法，并给出从双终点子样本估计转移率的方法。

Result: 证明所提再估计策略在保持一类错误率的同时，允许通过选择第二阶段样本量达到预设目标功效；推导出基于替代终点的条件功效与样本量规则，并用动机性试验示例加以说明。

Conclusion: 所提出的盲态样本量再估计方法能有效利用主要终点和替代终点信息，在控制一类错误的前提下实现目标功效，适用于配对二分类终点且存在短期替代终点的临床试验。

Abstract: We develop blinded sample size re-estimation strategies for McNemar's test based on paired binary primary and secondary short-term surrogate endpoints. The development is motivated by a prospective randomized clinical trial on childhood glaucoma.
